




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

abhidhammapiṭakai

paṭṭhānapāḷi

(catutthau bhāgau)

dhammānulaumai dukapaṭṭhānaṃ

12. kilaisagaucchakaṃ

75. kilaisadukaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. kilaisaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ thīnaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ, laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau diṭṭhi uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ, laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau mānau thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ, laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau mānau uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ, laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ, laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ; dausaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ, dausaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ; vicikicchaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ; uddhac‍caṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ| (1)

kilaisaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kilaisaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (2)

kilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau ca naukilaisau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ sampayuttakā ca khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ (cak‍kaṃ)| (3)

2. naukilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naukilaisaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naukilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naukilaisai khandhai paṭic‍ca kilaisā| (2)

naukilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau ca naukilaisau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – naukilaisaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kilaisā ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

3. kilaisañ‍ca naukilaisañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – laubhañ‍ca sampayuttakai ca khandhai paṭic‍ca mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ (cak‍kaṃ)| (1)

kilaisañ‍ca  naukilaisañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naukilaisaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca kilaisai ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… kilaisai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ  rūpaṃ| (2)

kilaisañ‍ca naukilaisañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau ca naukilaisau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – naukilaisaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca laubhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… (cak‍kaṃ, saṃkhittaṃ)| (3)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

4. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava (sabbattha nava), vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai nava|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

5. kilaisaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau, uddhac‍caṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau| (1)

naukilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naukilaisaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā)| (1)

naukilaisaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (2)

kilaisañ‍ca naukilaisañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca vicikicchañ‍ca paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau, uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca uddhac‍cañ‍ca paṭic‍ca uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayādi

6. kilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – kilaisai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naukilaisaṃ dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – naukilaisai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu (yāva asañ‍ñasattā)| (1)

kilaisañ‍ca naukilaisañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – kilaisai ca sampayuttakai ca khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, kilaisai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayā… naanantarapac‍cayā… nasamanantarapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… naupanissayapac‍cayā|

napuraijātapac‍cayādi

7. kilaisaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ, laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau diṭṭhi uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ, laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau mānau thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ, laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau mānau uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ, laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ  anauttappaṃ, laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ; vicikicchaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ; uddhac‍caṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ (arūpai dausamūlakaṃ natthi)| (1)

kilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai kilaisai paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā, kilaisai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ (aivaṃ navapi pañhā kātabbā), napacchājātapac‍cayā, naāsaivanapac‍cayā|

nakammapac‍cayau

8. kilaisaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – kilaisai paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā caitanā| (1)

naukilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – naukilaisai khandhai paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā caitanā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

kilaisañ‍ca naukilaisañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – kilaisai ca sampayuttakai ca khandhai paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā caitanā| (1) (aivaṃ sabbai pac‍cayā kātabbā|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

9. nahaituyā cattāri, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai  nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai  aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai nava, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

2. sahajātavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi sahajātavāraupi kātabbau|)

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

10. kilaisaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭic‍casadisā)|

naukilaisaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naukilaisaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… (yāva ajjhattikā mahābhūtā) vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naukilaisā khandhā| (1)

naukilaisaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  – naukilaisai khandhai pac‍cayā kilaisā, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kilaisā| (2)

naukilaisaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kilaisau ca naukilaisau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – naukilaisaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā kilaisā ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kilaisā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kilaisā ca sampayuttakā ca khandhā| (3)

11. kilaisañ‍ca naukilaisañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – laubhañ‍ca  sampayuttakai ca khandhai pac‍cayā mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ (cak‍kaṃ)| laubhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā kilaisā| (1)

kilaisañ‍ca naukilaisañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naukilaisaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca kilaisañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… kilaisai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, kilaisai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā naukilaisā khandhā| (2)

kilaisañ‍ca  naukilaisañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kilaisau ca naukilaisau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – naukilaisaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca laubhañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… (cak‍kaṃ)| laubhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ sampayuttakā ca khandhā (cak‍kaṃ)| (3)

(ārammaṇapac‍cayai naukilaisamūlai pañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇā kātabbā|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

12. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava (sabbattha nava), vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai nava|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

13. kilaisaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchaṃ pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau, uddhac‍caṃ pac‍cayā uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naukilaisaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) – cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā naukilaisā khandhā| (1)

naukilaisaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (2)

kilaisañ‍ca naukilaisañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchañ‍ca sampayuttakai ca khandhai vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau, uddhac‍cañ‍ca sampayuttakai ca khandhai vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

14. nahaituyā  cattāri, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava…pai॰… nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ…pai॰… nauvigatai tīṇi|

4. nissayavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi nissayavāraupi kātabbau|)

5. saṃsaṭṭhavārau

1-4. pac‍cayānulaumādi

15. kilaisaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  – laubhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ| (cak‍kaṃ| aivaṃ nava pañhā kātabbā|)

haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai nava (sabbattha nava), vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai nava|

anulaumaṃ|

kilaisaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā (aivaṃ nahaitupañhā cattāri kātabbā|)

nahaituyā cattāri, naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai nava|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

6. sampayuttavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi sampayuttavāraupi kātabbau|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

16. kilaisau dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kilaisā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ kilaisānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ  pucchitabbaṃ|) kilaisā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ pucchitabbaṃ|) kilaisā  haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kilaisānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naukilaisau dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naukilaisā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

17. kilaisau dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – kilaisai ārabbha kilaisā uppaj‍janti| (mūlaṃ pucchitabbaṃ|) kilaisai ārabbha naukilaisā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (mūlaṃ pucchitabbaṃ|) kilaisai ārabbha kilaisā ca sampayuttakā ca khandhā uppaj‍janti| (3)

18. naukilaisau dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau…pai॰… diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… phalassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ naukilaisai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naukilaisau dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau…pai॰… diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, jhānai parihīnai vippaṭisārissa daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ naukilaisai khandhai assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (2)

naukilaisau dhammau kilaisassa ca naukilaisassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ naukilaisai khandhai assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha kilaisā ca sampayuttakā ca khandhā uppaj‍janti| (3)

kilaisau  ca naukilaisau ca dhammā kilaisassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

adhipatipac‍cayau

19. kilaisau dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – kilaisai garuṃ katvā kilaisā uppaj‍janti… tīṇi (ārammaṇādhipatiyaiva)| (3)

naukilaisau dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati; pubbai…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti…pai॰… phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ naukilaisai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – naukilaisādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naukilaisau dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… jhānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ naukilaisai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – naukilaisādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ kilaisānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naukilaisau dhammau kilaisassa ca naukilaisassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati  – dānaṃ…pai॰… jhānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ naukilaisai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā kilaisā ca sampayuttakā ca khandhā uppaj‍janti| sahajātādhipati – naukilaisādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kilaisānañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

kilaisau  ca naukilaisau ca dhammā kilaisassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (ārammaṇādhipatiyaiva)| (3)

anantarapac‍cayādi

20. kilaisau  dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā kilaisā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kilaisānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) purimā purimā kilaisā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ naukilaisānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; kilaisā vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) purimā purimā kilaisā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kilaisānaṃ sampayuttakānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

21. naukilaisau dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā naukilaisā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ naukilaisānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) purimā purimā naukilaisā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kilaisānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; āvaj‍janā kilaisānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) purimā purimā naukilaisā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kilaisānaṃ sampayuttakānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; āvaj‍janā kilaisānaṃ sampayuttakānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ  anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

22. kilaisau ca naukilaisau ca dhammā kilaisassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā kilaisā ca sampayuttakā ca khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kilaisānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) purimā purimā kilaisā ca sampayuttakā ca khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ naukilaisānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; kilaisā ca sampayuttakā ca khandhā vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) purimā purimā kilaisā ca sampayuttakā ca khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kilaisānaṃ sampayuttakānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

samanantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau, sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

upanissayapac‍cayau

23. kilaisau  dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – kilaisā kilaisānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

24. naukilaisau dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ… dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ saddhāya…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naukilaisau  dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya mānaṃ jappaiti… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; sīlaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ kilaisānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naukilaisau dhammau kilaisassa ca naukilaisassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya mānaṃ jappaiti… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; sīlaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ kilaisānaṃ sampayuttakānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

kilaisau  ca naukilaisau ca dhammā kilaisassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

puraijātapac‍cayau

25. naukilaisau dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbaina cakkhunā  rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰…| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu naukilaisānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naukilaisau dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu kilaisānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naukilaisau dhammau kilaisassa ca naukilaisassa ca dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha kilaisā ca sampayuttakā ca khandhā uppaj‍janti| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu kilaisānaṃ sampayuttakānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

pacchājātāsaivanapac‍cayā

26. kilaisau dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

naukilaisau dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

kilaisau ca naukilaisau ca dhammā naukilaisassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ) āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau …nava|

kammapac‍cayau

27. naukilaisau  dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – naukilaisā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – naukilaisā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naukilaisau  dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naukilaisā caitanā kilaisānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naukilaisau dhammau kilaisassa ca naukilaisassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naukilaisā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ  khandhānaṃ kilaisānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

vipākapac‍cayādi

28. naukilaisau dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… aikaṃ… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

29. kilaisau dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

naukilaisau dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

naukilaisau  dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu kilaisānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naukilaisau dhammau kilaisassa ca naukilaisassa ca dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu kilaisānaṃ sampayuttakānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

kilaisau ca naukilaisau ca dhammā naukilaisassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ| vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

atthipac‍cayādi

30. kilaisau dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… aikaṃ (paṭic‍casadisaṃ)| (1)

kilaisau dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (2)

kilaisau  dhammau kilaisassa ca naukilaisassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (paṭic‍casadisaṃ)| (3)

naukilaisau dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

naukilaisau dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātaṃ sahajātasadisaṃ, puraijātaṃ puraijātasadisaṃ|) (2)

naukilaisau dhammau kilaisassa ca naukilaisassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ (sahajātaṃ sahajātasadisaṃ, puraijātaṃ puraijātasadisaṃ|) (3)

31. kilaisau  ca naukilaisau ca dhammā kilaisassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – laubhau ca sampayuttakā ca khandhā mauhassa, diṭṭhiyā, thinassa, uddhac‍cassa, ahirikassa, anauttappassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (cak‍kaṃ)| sahajātau – laubhau ca vatthu ca mauhassa, diṭṭhiyā, thinassa, uddhac‍cassa, ahirikassa, anauttappassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (cak‍kaṃ)| (1)

kilaisau ca naukilaisau ca dhammā naukilaisassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātau – naukilaisau aikau khandhau ca kilaisau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca…pai॰…| sahajātā – kilaisā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātā – kilaisā ca vatthu ca naukilaisānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – kilaisā ca sampayuttakā  khandhā ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – kilaisā ca sampayuttakā ca khandhā kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – kilaisā ca sampayuttakā ca khandhā rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

kilaisau ca naukilaisau ca dhammā kilaisassa ca naukilaisassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau  – naukilaisau aikau khandhau ca laubhau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ mauhassa ca, diṭṭhiyā, thinassa, uddhac‍cassa, ahirikassa, anauttappassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca…pai॰…| sahajātau – laubhau ca vatthu ca mauhassa, diṭṭhiyā, thinassa, uddhac‍cassa, ahirikassa, anauttappassa sampayuttakānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (cak‍kaṃ)| (3)

natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

32. haituyā  cattāri, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

33. kilaisau dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kilaisau  dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

kilaisau dhammau kilaisassa ca naukilaisassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

34. naukilaisau dhammau naukilaisassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau … puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naukilaisau dhammau kilaisassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naukilaisau  dhammau kilaisassa ca naukilaisassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

35. kilaisau ca naukilaisau ca dhammā kilaisassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kilaisau ca naukilaisau ca dhammā naukilaisassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

kilaisau ca naukilaisau ca dhammā kilaisassa ca naukilaisassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

36. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava, naadhipatiyā nava (sabbattha nava), nauavigatai nava|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

37. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cattāri, naadhipatiyā cattāri, naanantarai cattāri, nasamanantarai cattāri, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, naupanissayai cattāri (sabbattha cattāri), nasampayuttai dvai, navippayuttai cattāri, naunatthiyā cattāri, nauvigatai cattāri|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

38. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava (anulaumamātikā kātabbā)…pai॰… avigatai nava|

kilaisadukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

76. saṃkilaisikadukaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

39. saṃkilaisikaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (yathā laukiyadukaṃ, aivaṃ nin‍nānākaraṇaṃ|)

haitupac‍cayau

40. saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ  paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca  asaṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

41. haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai dvai, samanantarai dvai, sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai pañ‍ca, upanissayai dvai, puraijātai dvai, āsaivanai dvai, kammai pañ‍ca, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ), avigatai  pañ‍ca|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

42. saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā)| (1)

1. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

43. nahaituyā  dvai, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā pañ‍ca, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai cattāri, napacchājātai pañ‍ca, naāsaivanai pañ‍ca, nakammai dvai, navipākai pañ‍ca, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

2. sahajātavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi sahajātavāraupi kātabbau|)

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

44. saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭic‍casadisaṃ)|

asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva ajjhattikā mahābhūtā) vatthuṃ pac‍cayā asaṃkiliṭṭhā khandhā| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saṃkiliṭṭhau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā saṃkiliṭṭhā khandhā| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saṃkiliṭṭhau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā saṃkiliṭṭhā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

45. saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saṃkiliṭṭhau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… saṃkiliṭṭhai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (3)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

46. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cattāri, nissayai nava, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai cattāri, āsaivanai cattāri, kammai nava, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai nava, indriyai nava…pai॰… vigatai cattāri, avigatai nava|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

47. saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau  uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā asaṃkiliṭṭhā khandhā| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

48. nahaituyā cattāri, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai cattāri, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai cattāri, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

4. nissayavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi nissayavāraupi kātabbau|)

5. saṃsaṭṭhavārau

1-4. pac‍cayānulaumādi

haitupac‍cayau

49. saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai  …pai॰…| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

haituyā dvai, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā dvai (sabbattha dvai), vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai dvai|

anulaumaṃ|

50. saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

nahaituyā dvai, naadhipatiyā dvai, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, nakammai dvai, navipākai dvai, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

6. sampayuttavārau

(aivaṃ  itarai dvai gaṇanāpi sampayuttavāraupi kātabbau|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

51. saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – saṃkiliṭṭhā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – asaṃkiliṭṭhā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

52. saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – rāgaṃ assādaiti  abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati; vicikicchā…pai॰… uddhac‍ca…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; diṭṭhiṃ assādaiti…pai॰… (kusalattikasadisaṃ); vicikicchaṃ ārabbha…pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ ārabbha…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai…pai॰… pubbai samudāciṇṇai…pai॰… saṃkiliṭṭhai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, caitaupariyañāṇaina saṃkiliṭṭhacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti; saṃkiliṭṭhā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

53. asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… pubbai…pai॰… jhānā  vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti…pai॰… āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhai khandhai  assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (2)

adhipatipac‍cayau

54. saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – rāgaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati; diṭṭhiṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti  abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – saṃkiliṭṭhādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – saṃkiliṭṭhādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – saṃkiliṭṭhādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

55. asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa  adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… pubbai…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti…pai॰… nibbānaṃ phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – asaṃkiliṭṭhādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… pubbai…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

anantarapac‍cayādi

56. saṃkiliṭṭhau  dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā saṃkiliṭṭhā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhā khandhā vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

57. asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā asaṃkiliṭṭhā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – āvaj‍janā  saṃkiliṭṭhānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

upanissayapac‍cayau

58. saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – rāgaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati, dausaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati, rāgau…pai॰… patthanā rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – rāgaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, dausaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, rāgau…pai॰… patthanā saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa… maggassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

59. asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ  uppādaiti; sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ, kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ  uppādaiti; saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ saddhāya…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

puraijātapac‍cayau

60. asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (2)

pacchājātāsaivanapac‍cayā

61. saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)| asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)| …āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

kammapac‍cayau

62. saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – saṃkiliṭṭhā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – saṃkiliṭṭhā  caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) saṃkiliṭṭhā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

asaṃkiliṭṭhau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – asaṃkiliṭṭhā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – asaṃkiliṭṭhā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākapac‍cayādi

63. asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… aikaṃ|

saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

64. saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu saṃkiliṭṭhānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

atthipac‍cayādi

65. saṃkiliṭṭhau  dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau … aikaṃ (paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ)| saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (paṭic‍casadisaṃ)| (3)

asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – puraijātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (2)

66. saṃkiliṭṭhau  ca asaṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammā saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – saṃkiliṭṭhau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca…pai॰… (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammā asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – saṃkiliṭṭhā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – saṃkiliṭṭhā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – saṃkiliṭṭhā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

67. haituyā cattāri, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai  cattāri, samanantarai  cattāri, sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai satta, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai dvai, pacchājātai dvai, āsaivanai dvai, kammai cattāri, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai cattāri, indriyai cattāri, jhānai cattāri, maggai cattāri, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā satta, natthiyā cattāri, vigatai cattāri, avigatai satta|

2. pac‍canīyuddhārau

68. saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhau  dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

69. asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammā saṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ… puraijātaṃ| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhau  ca asaṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammā asaṃkiliṭṭhassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ… pacchājātaṃ… āhāraṃ… indriyaṃ| (2)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

70. nahaituyā satta, naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai satta, nasahajātai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, nanissayai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai cha, napacchājātai satta…pai॰… namaggai satta, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai cattāri, nauatthiyā cattāri, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta, nauavigatai cattāri|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

71. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cattāri, naadhipatiyā cattāri, naanantarai cattāri, nasamanantarai cattāri, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, naupanissayai cattāri…pai॰… nasampayuttai dvai, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā cattāri, nauvigatai cattāri|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

72. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca (anulaumamātikā)…pai॰… avigatai satta|

saṃkiliṭṭhadukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

78. kilaisasampayuttadukaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

73. kilaisasampayuttaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kilaisasampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

kilaisasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisavippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kilaisasampayuttai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

(kilaisasampayuttadukaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhadukasadisaṃ, nin‍nānākaraṇaṃ|)

kilaisasampayuttadukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

79. kilaisasaṃkilaisikadukaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

haitupac‍cayau

74. kilaisañ‍caiva saṃkilaisikañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau caiva saṃkilaisikau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ (cak‍kaṃ)| (1)

kilaisañ‍caiva saṃkilaisikañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkilaisikau caiva nau ca kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kilaisai paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (2)

kilaisañ‍caiva saṃkilaisikañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau  caiva saṃkilaisikau ca saṃkilaisikau caiva nau ca kilaisau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ sampayuttakā ca khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

2-6. sahajāta-pac‍caya-nissaya-saṃsaṭṭha-sampayuttavārau

(aivaṃ  paṭic‍cavāraupi sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi kilaisadukasadisā| nin‍nānākaraṇaṃ| āmasanaṃ nānaṃ|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

75. kilaisau caiva saṃkilaisikau ca dhammau kilaisassa caiva saṃkilaisikassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kilaisā caiva saṃkilaisikā ca haitū sampayuttakānaṃ kilaisānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (aivaṃ cattāri, kilaisadukasadisaṃ|) (4)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

76. kilaisau caiva saṃkilaisikau ca dhammau kilaisassa caiva saṃkilaisikassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kilaisai ārabbha kilaisā uppaj‍janti| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) kilaisai ārabbha saṃkilaisikā caiva nau ca kilaisā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) kilaisai ārabbha kilaisā ca sampayuttakā ca khandhā uppaj‍janti| (3)

77. saṃkilaisikau caiva nau ca kilaisau dhammau saṃkilaisikassa caiva nau ca kilaisassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, jhānai parihīnai  vippaṭisārissa daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; ariyā gautrabhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ saṃkilaisikai caiva nau ca kilaisai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; dibbaina cakkhunā  rūpaṃ passati…pai॰… āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (itarai dvai kilaisadukasadisā, ghaṭanārammaṇāpi kilaisadukasadisā)|

adhipatipac‍cayau

78. kilaisau  caiva saṃkilaisikau ca dhammau kilaisassa caiva saṃkilaisikassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati… tīṇi|

saṃkilaisikau caiva nau ca kilaisau dhammau saṃkilaisikassa caiva nau ca kilaisassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, saikkhā gautrabhuṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti; cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ saṃkilaisikai caiva nau ca kilaisai khandhai garuṃ katvā saṃkilaisikā caiva nau ca kilaisā khandhā uppaj‍janti| sahajātādhipati – saṃkilaisikā caiva nau ca kilaisādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (itarai dvaipi kilaisadukasadisā| ghaṭanādhipatipi|)

anantarapac‍cayādi

79. kilaisau caiva saṃkilaisikau ca dhammau kilaisassa caiva saṃkilaisikassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (kilaisadukasadisā)|

saṃkilaisikau caiva nau ca kilaisau dhammau saṃkilaisikassa caiva nau ca kilaisassa dhammassa  anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā saṃkilaisikā caiva nau ca kilaisā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ saṃkilaisikānañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… āvaj‍janā saṃkilaisikānañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

(itarai dvai anantarā kilaisadukasadisā, nin‍nānākaraṇā| ghaṭanānantarampi sabbai pac‍cayā kilaisadukasadisā, nin‍nānākaraṇā| upanissayai laukuttaraṃ natthi, idaṃ dukaṃ kilaisadukasadisaṃ, nin‍nānākaraṇaṃ|)

kilaisasaṃkilaisikadukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

80. kilaisasaṃkiliṭṭhadukaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

80. kilaisañ‍caiva  saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ (cak‍kaṃ)| (1)

kilaisañ‍caiva saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhau caiva nau ca kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kilaisai paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā| (2)

kilaisañ‍caiva saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca saṃkiliṭṭhau caiva nau ca kilaisau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – laubhaṃ paṭic‍ca mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ sampayuttakā ca khandhā  (cak‍kaṃ)| (3)

81. saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhau caiva nau ca kilaisau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhai caiva nau ca kilaisai khandhai paṭic‍ca kilaisā| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca saṃkiliṭṭhau caiva nau ca kilaisau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kilaisā ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

82. kilaisañ‍caiva saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – laubhañ‍ca sampayuttakai ca khandhai paṭic‍ca mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ (cak‍kaṃ)| (1)

kilaisañ‍caiva  saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhau caiva nau ca kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca kilaisai ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| (2)

kilaisañ‍caiva saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca saṃkiliṭṭhau caiva nau ca kilaisau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca laubhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā mauhau diṭṭhi thinaṃ uddhac‍caṃ ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… (cak‍kaṃ)| (3)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

83. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai nava (sabbattha nava), kammai nava, āhārai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

84. kilaisañ‍caiva  saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchaṃ paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau, uddhac‍caṃ paṭic‍ca uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

kilaisañ‍caiva saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vicikicchañ‍ca uddhac‍cañ‍ca paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

85. nahaituyā  tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, navippayuttai nava|

2-6. sahajāta-pac‍caya-nissaya-saṃsaṭṭha-sampayuttavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

86. kilaisau  caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammau kilaisassa caiva saṃkiliṭṭhassa ca dhammassa  haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kilaisā caiva saṃkiliṭṭhā ca haitū sampayuttakānaṃ kilaisānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa caiva nau ca kilaisassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kilaisā caiva saṃkiliṭṭhā ca haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammau kilaisassa caiva saṃkiliṭṭhassa ca saṃkiliṭṭhassa caiva nau ca kilaisassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kilaisā caiva saṃkiliṭṭhā ca haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kilaisānañ‍ca haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

87. kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammau kilaisassa caiva saṃkiliṭṭhassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kilaisai ārabbha kilaisā uppaj‍janti| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) kilaisai ārabbha saṃkiliṭṭhā caiva nau ca kilaisā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) kilaisai ārabbha kilaisā ca sampayuttakā khandhā ca uppaj‍janti| (3)

88. saṃkiliṭṭhau  caiva nau ca kilaisau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa caiva nau ca kilaisassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhai caiva nau ca kilaisai khandhai ārabbha saṃkiliṭṭhā  caiva nau ca kilaisā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) saṃkiliṭṭhai caiva nau ca kilaisai khandhai ārabbha kilaisā uppaj‍janti| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) saṃkiliṭṭhai caiva nau ca kilaisai khandhai ārabbha kilaisā ca sampayuttakā khandhā ca uppaj‍janti| (3)

(itaraipi tīṇi kātabbā|)

adhipatipac‍cayau

89. kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammau kilaisassa caiva saṃkiliṭṭhassa ca  dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati… tīṇi|

saṃkiliṭṭhau caiva nau ca kilaisau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa caiva nau ca kilaisassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – saṃkiliṭṭhai caiva nau ca kilaisai khandhai garuṃ katvā…pai॰… tīṇi| (dvai adhipati tīṇipi kātabbā, itarai dvaipi tīṇi kātabbā|)

anantarapac‍cayādi

90. kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca dhammau kilaisassa caiva saṃkiliṭṭhassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (navapi kātabbā, āvaj‍janāpi vuṭṭhānampi natthi)… samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava pañhā (puraijātapac‍cayau pacchājātapac‍cayaupi natthi)… āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

kammapac‍cayādi

91. saṃkiliṭṭhau caiva nau ca kilaisau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhassa caiva nau ca kilaisassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhā caiva nau ca kilaisā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhau  caiva nau ca kilaisau dhammau kilaisassa caiva saṃkiliṭṭhassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhā caiva nau  ca kilaisā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ kilaisānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhau caiva nau ca kilaisau dhammau kilaisassa caiva saṃkiliṭṭhassa ca saṃkiliṭṭhassa caiva nau ca kilaisassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhā caiva nau ca kilaisā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kilaisānañ‍ca kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

āhārapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… tīṇi… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava… atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava… natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|
