2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

10. nahaituyā cattāri, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai  tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai cattāri, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai cattāri, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

4. nissayavārau

(itarai dvai gaṇanāpi nissayavāraupi kātabbau|)

5. saṃsaṭṭhavārau

1-4. pac‍cayānulaumādi

11. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

nadassanaina  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

haituyā dvai, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā dvai, avigatai dvai|

anulaumaṃ|

12. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (1)

nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

nahaituyā  dvai, naadhipatiyā dvai, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, nakammai dvai, navipākai dvai, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

6. sampayuttavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi sampayuttavāraupi kātabbau|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

13. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – nadassanaina pahātabbā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

14. dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā  uppaj‍jati, dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; dassanaina pahātabbaṃ diṭṭhiṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; vicikicchaṃ ārabbha vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā dassanaina pahātabbai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai…pai॰… dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, caitaupariyañāṇaina dassanaina pahātabbacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

15. nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha nadassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati; uddhac‍caṃ…pai॰… nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā …pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti…pai॰… phalassa āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; ariyā nadassanaina pahātabbai pahīnai kilaisai  pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai…pai॰… pubbai…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nadassanaina pahātabbai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha nadassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ…pai॰… nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nadassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… pubbai…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… cakkhuṃ …pai॰… vatthuṃ nadassanaina pahātabbai khandhai assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (2)

adhipatipac‍cayau

16. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhiṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰…| sahajātādhipati – dassanaina pahātabbādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – dassanaina pahātabbādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau  dassanaina pahātabbassa ca nadassanaina pahātabbassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – dassanaina pahātabbādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

17. nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… pubbai…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā nadassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nadassanaina pahātabbai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā nadassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – nadassanaina pahātabbādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nadassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā…pai॰… jhānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nadassanaina pahātabbai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

anantarapac‍cayau

18. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ dassanaina pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) purimā purimā nadassanaina pahātabbā khandhā…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) āvaj‍janā dassanaina pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

upanissayapac‍cayau

19. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau…pai॰… patthanā dassanaina pahātabbassa rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya  dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti; dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau…pai॰… patthanā saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya nadassanaina pahātabbassa rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… mānassa … patthanāya… kāyikassa sukhassa…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

20. nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti; sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ, nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ… dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti; saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā, nadassanaina pahātabbau rāgau…pai॰… patthanā… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya nadassanaina pahātabbassa rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya… kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa… maggassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti, nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ… dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ dassanaina pahātabbassa rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

puraijātapac‍cayau

21. nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa  puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha nadassanaina pahātabbau rāgau …pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ…pai॰… nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰…| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa  …pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu nadassanaina pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau…pai॰… diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu dassanaina pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pacchājātāsaivanapac‍cayā

22. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)… āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

kammapac‍cayau

23. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau  nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – dassanaina pahātabbā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – dassanaina pahātabbā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa ca nadassanaina pahātabbassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

24. nadassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – nadassanaina pahātabbā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – nadassanaina pahātabbā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

vipākapac‍cayādi

25. nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… aikaṃ… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

26. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

nadassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu dassanaina pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

atthipac‍cayādi

27. dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (paṭic‍casadisaṃ)| dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa ca nadassanaina pahātabbassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (paṭic‍cavārasadisā)| (3)

nadassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… (saṃkhittaṃ, puraijātasadisaṃ)| (2)

28. dassanaina pahātabbau ca nadassanaina pahātabbau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – dassanaina pahātabbau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau ca nadassanaina pahātabbau ca dhammā nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

29. haituyā  cattāri, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai satta, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai dvai, pacchājātai dvai, āsaivanai dvai, kammai cattāri, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai cattāri, indriyai cattāri, jhānai cattāri, maggai cattāri, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā satta, natthiyā cattāri, vigatai cattāri, avigatai satta|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

30. dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa ca nadassanaina pahātabbassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

31. nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa  dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nadassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbau ca nadassanaina pahātabbau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau ca nadassanaina pahātabbau ca dhammā nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (2)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

32. nahaituyā satta, naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai satta, nasahajātai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, nanissayai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai cha, napacchājātai satta…pai॰… namaggai satta, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai cattāri, nauatthiyā cattāri, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta, nauavigatai cattāri|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

33. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai cattāri, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai  cattāri, nasamanantarai cattāri, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, naupanissayai cattāri (sabbattha cattāri), nasampayuttai dvai, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā cattāri, nauvigatai cattāri|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

34. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca (anulaumamātikā kātabbā)…pai॰… avigatai satta|

dassanainapahātabbadukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

84. bhāvanāyapahātabbadukaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

1-4. pac‍cayānulaumādi

35. bhāvanāya  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (yathā dassanadukaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ, nin‍nānākaraṇaṃ)|

haituyā pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca|

anulaumaṃ|

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau|

nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā  dvai…pai॰… nauvigatai tīṇi|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

(pac‍cayavārapac‍canīyai  nahaitupac‍cayai uddhac‍casahagatai tīṇi, mauhau uddharitabbau| sabbaipi vārā dassanadukasadisā, uddhac‍capac‍canīyampi nānaṃ|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

36. bhāvanāya  pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

37. bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati; bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; uddhac‍caṃ ārabbha uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā bhāvanāya pahātabbai pahīnai kilaisai…pai॰… vikkhambhitai kilaisai…pai॰… pubbai samudāciṇṇai…pai॰… bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha nabhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; caitaupariyañāṇaina  bhāvanāya pahātabbacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, bhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

38. nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha nabhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, pubbai…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… phalassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; ariyā nabhāvanāya pahātabbai pahīnai kilaisai…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha nabhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… jhānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (2)

adhipatipac‍cayau

39. bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā nabhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi  uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

40. nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā nabhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – nabhāvanāya pahātabbādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa  dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… jhānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati| (2)

anantarapac‍cayādi

41. bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri (dassanadukasadisā bhāvanā nin‍nānākaraṇā)… samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

upanissayapac‍cayau

42. bhāvanāya  pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau… dausau… mauhau… mānau… patthanā bhāvanāya pahātabbassa rāgassa … dausassa… mauhassa… mānassa… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau…pai॰… patthanā saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya  nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa, rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya… kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa… maggassa… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

43. nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ, nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ… dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya… kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa… maggassa… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya mānaṃ jappaiti…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ…pai॰… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya mānaṃ jappaiti; saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbassa rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… mānassa… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

puraijātapac‍cayādi

44. nabhāvanāya  pahātabbau dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa  puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha nabhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati; nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰…| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu nabhāvanāya pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu bhāvanāya pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

45. pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai, āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai, kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) bhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) bhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

nabhāvanāya  pahātabbau  dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – nabhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – nabhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (sabbapac‍cayā dassanadukasadisā, bhāvanā nin‍nānākaraṇā|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

46. haituyā  cattāri, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai satta, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai dvai, pacchājātai dvai, āsaivanai dvai, kammai cattāri, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai cattāri, indriyai cattāri, jhānai cattāri, maggai cattāri, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā satta, natthiyā cattāri, vigatai cattāri, avigatai satta|

(pac‍canīyavibhaṅgau dassanadukasadisau vibhajitabbau| aivaṃ tīṇi gaṇanāpi gaṇaitabbā|)

bhāvanāyapahātabbadukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

85. dassanainapahātabbahaitukadukaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

47. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

48. nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva ajjhattikā mahābhūtā)| (1)

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā| (2)

nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau  ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

49. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

50. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā mauhau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

51. nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

52. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai cha, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai cha, samanantarai cha, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nissayai nava, upanissayai cha, puraijātai cha, āsaivanai cha, kammai nava, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai nava, indriyai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai cha, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā cha, vigatai cha, avigatai nava|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

53. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai  paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (1)

nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā ahaitukaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

54. nahaituyā dvai, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai cattāri, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai cha, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

55. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava…pai॰… napuraijātai satta…pai॰… navippayuttai cattāri, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

56. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai dvai, anantarai dvai…pai॰… vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… maggai dvai…pai॰… avigatai dvai|

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

57. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva ajjhattikā mahābhūtā) vatthuṃ pac‍cayā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā| (1)

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā, vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā| (2)

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

58. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

59. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭic‍casadisā)|

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā, vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā| nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca| (3)

60. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau  dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai  khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā mauhau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| (3)

2. saṅkhyāvārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

62. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (1)

nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā, uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

68. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ  cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

69 . nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca  rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

70. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ārabbha nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ārabbha vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (3)

71. nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, pubbai suciṇṇāni  …pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti…pai॰… ariyā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai…pai॰… pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti, cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya, mauhassa ca ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… jhānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau  rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati|

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca ārabbha vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (3)

72. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vicikicchāsahagatai  khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca ārabbha nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca ārabbha vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (3)

adhipatipac‍cayau

73. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai garuṃ katvā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā uppaj‍janti| sahajātādhipati – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa  ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

74. nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā  taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… phalassa  adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

anantarapac‍cayau

75. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ dassanaina pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) purimā purimā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā pacchimassa pacchimassa vicikicchāsahagatassa mauhassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) purimā purimā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

76. nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau pacchimassa pacchimassa vicikicchāsahagatassa mauhassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; purimā purimā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) purimau purimau vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; āvaj‍janā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) purimau purimau vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ  vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; āvaj‍janā vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

77. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) purimā purimā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca pacchimassa pacchimassa vicikicchāsahagatassa mauhassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) purimā purimā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ  vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cha… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

upanissayapac‍cayau

78. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (avasaisaisu dvīsu anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau|) (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

79. nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti mānaṃ jappaiti; sīlaṃ  …pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ rāgaṃ… dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti; saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa  rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ rāgaṃ… dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā, nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau… dausau… mauhau… mānau… patthanā… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ, vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

80. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau  ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

puraijātapac‍cayau

81. nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; dibbaina…pai॰…| (saṃkhittaṃ| vatthupuraijātaṃ saṃkhittaṃ|) (1)

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (vatthupuraijātaṃ saṃkhittaṃ|) (2)

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ ārabbha vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (vatthupuraijātaṃ saṃkhittaṃ|) (3)

pacchājātāsaivanapac‍cayā

82. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)| nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)| dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)… āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

kammapac‍cayādi

83. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā caitanā  sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cha… vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca|

atthipac‍cayādi

84. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (3)

85. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatau aikau khandhau ca mauhau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca…pai॰…| dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa  dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (3)

natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

86. haituyā  cha, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai nava, kammai cattāri, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai cattāri, indriyai cattāri, jhānai cattāri, maggai cattāri, sampayuttai cha, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

87. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa  ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

88. nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

89. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā  nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

90. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (sabbattha nava), nauavigatai nava|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

91. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai cha, naadhipatiyā cha, naanantarai cha, nasamanantarai cha, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, naupanissayai cha…pai॰… nasampayuttai dvai, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā cha, nauvigatai cha|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

92. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca (anulaumamātikā)…pai॰… avigatai nava|

dassanainapahātabbahaitukadukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

86. bhāvanāyapahātabbahaitukadukaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

93. bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…|

2-6. sahajāta-pac‍caya-nissaya-saṃsaṭṭha-sampayuttavārau

(aivaṃ paṭic‍cavāraupi sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi dassanaina pahātabbahaitukadukasadisā| uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau vicikicchāsahagatamauhaṭṭhānai ṭhapaitabbau|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

94. bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cha…pai॰… (dassanaina pahātabbahaitukadukasadisā)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

95. bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbahaitukadukasadisā)|

96. nabhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… ariyā maggā…pai॰… phalassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; ariyā nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai pahīnai kilaisai…pai॰… pubbai samudāciṇṇai…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya, mauhassa ca ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… jhānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ…pai॰… bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (2)

nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca ārabbha uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti (ghaṭanārammaṇā tīṇipi kātabbā)| (3)

adhipatipac‍cayādi

97. bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ rāgaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau  rāgau uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ rāgaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ  rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

98. nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, pubbai…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nabhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… jhānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati| (2)

(anantarapac‍cayai  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukakāraṇā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau na kātabbau, uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau kātabbau|) samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cha… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

upanissayapac‍cayau

99. bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; sakabhaṇḍai chandarāgau parabhaṇḍai chandarāgassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; sakapariggahai chandarāgau parapariggahai chandarāgassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā uddhac‍casahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

100. nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ rāgaṃ… dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ  sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya… nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa rāgassa … dausassa… mauhassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya… kāyikassa sukhassa…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya mānaṃ jappaiti…pai॰… saddhā …pai॰… saināsanaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… mānassa… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ uddhac‍casahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (ghaṭanūpanissayāpi tīṇipi kātabbā)| (3)

puraijātapac‍cayādi

101. nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi… āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (nabhāvanāya pahātabbabhājanakāraṇai nānākkhaṇikā labbhati ) …pai॰… nauvigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ| yathā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukadukaṃ aivaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukapac‍cayāpi pac‍canīyāpi vibhāgaupi gaṇanāpi nin‍nānākaraṇā|)

nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau nadassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa…pai॰…| (parantaina sakabhaṇḍachandarāgaupi kātabbau|)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa…pai॰…| (parantaina ‘‘sakabhaṇḍachandarāgau’’ti kātabbaṃ|)

bhāvanāyapahātabbahaitukadukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

87. savitak‍kadukaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

102. savitak‍kaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| savitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kai khandhai paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| savitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau ca avitak‍kau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

103. avitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – avitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau  khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā, vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā, paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kā khandhā| (2)

avitak‍kaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau ca avitak‍kau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kā khandhā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kā khandhā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kā khandhā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau sampayuttakā ca khandhā| (3)

104. savitak‍kañ‍ca avitak‍kañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai  …pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca  vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kañ‍ca avitak‍kañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau| (2)

savitak‍kañ‍ca avitak‍kañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau ca avitak‍kau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… savitak‍kai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… savitak‍kai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

105. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… upanissayai nava, puraijātai cha, āsaivanai cha, kammai nava, vipākai nava (sabbattha nava), avigatai nava|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

106. savitak‍kaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca  vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (savitak‍kamūlakā avasaisā dvai pañhā kātabbā, ahaitukaṃ nin‍nānaṃ|) (3)

avitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ avitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukaṃ vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā, ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukā savitak‍kā khandhā, vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (2)

avitak‍kaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau ca avitak‍kau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ| haitupac‍cayasadisaṃ| ‘‘ahaituka’’nti niyāmaitabbaṃ|) (3)

savitak‍kañ‍ca avitak‍kañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau  khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (avasaisā dvai pañhā haitupac‍cayasadisā nin‍nānā, ahaitukanti niyāmaitabbaṃ|) (3)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

107. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi…pai॰… naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai cattāri, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai nava, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai cha, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

108. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi…pai॰… nakammai cattāri, navipākai nava, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai cha, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

109. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… anantarai nava…pai॰… puraijātai cha, āsaivanai pañ‍ca, kammai nava…pai॰… maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai nava (sabbattha nava)|

2. sahajātavārau

(sahajātavārau paṭic‍cavārasadisau|)

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

110. savitak‍kaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭic‍cavārasadisā)|

avitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – avitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vitak‍kaṃ pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kaṃ pac‍cayā kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā, vitak‍kaṃ pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vitak‍kau, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kā khandhā, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vitak‍kau|

avitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vitak‍kaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kā khandhā (paṭisandhiyāpi dvai)|

avitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kau ca avitak‍kau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vitak‍kaṃ pac‍cayā  sampayuttakā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, vitak‍kaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kā khandhā, mahābhūtai  pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vitak‍kau sampayuttakā ca khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (paṭisandhiyāpi pavattisadisāyaiva)| (3)

111. savitak‍kañ‍ca avitak‍kañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… (paṭisandhikkhaṇai dvai  kātabbā)| (1)

savitak‍kañ‍ca avitak‍kañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, savitak‍kai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, savitak‍kai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vitak‍kau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (tīṇi, paṭisandhiyāpi)| (2)

savitak‍kañ‍ca avitak‍kañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kau ca avitak‍kau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… savitak‍kai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

115. savitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau savitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| savitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau avitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau vitak‍kau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| savitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau savitak‍kau ca avitak‍kau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

avitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau avitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – avitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| avitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ  saṃsaṭṭhau savitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vitak‍kaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā sampayuttakā khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kañ‍ca avitak‍kañ‍ca dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau savitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

anulaumaṃ|

nahaituyā cha, naadhipatiyā cha, napuraijātai cha, napacchājātai cha, naāsaivanai cha, nakammai cattāri, navipākai cha, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai cha, navippayuttai cha|
