haituyā nava, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai  nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nissayai nava, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai cattāri, āsaivanai cattāri, kammai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava|

anulaumaṃ|

nahaitupac‍cayādi

203. kāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ  pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā kāmāvacarā khandhā, vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naadhipatipac‍cayādi

204. kāmāvacaraṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā… aikaṃ (yāva asañ‍ñasattā)| kāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nakāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā nakāmāvacarādhipati, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā nakāmāvacarā khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| kāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kāmāvacarau ca nakāmāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā nakāmāvacarā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

nakāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nakāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭic‍casadisā)|

kāmāvacarañ‍ca  nakāmāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (paṭic‍casadisā| mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) nakāmāvacarai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā nakāmāvacarādhipati, vipākaṃ nakāmāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) vipākaṃ nakāmāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… vipākai nakāmāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

naupanissayapac‍cayā… tīṇi… naāsaivanapac‍cayā (suddhakai arūpamissakai ca  ‘‘vipāka’’nti niyāmaitabbaṃ, rūpamissakai natthi| saṃkhittaṃ)|

suddhaṃ

nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai cattāri, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

4. nissayavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi nissayavāraupi kātabbau|)

5. saṃsaṭṭhavārau

1-4. pac‍cayānulaumādi

haitupac‍cayau

205. kāmāvacaraṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kāmāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

nakāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau nakāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – nakāmāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

haituyā dvai, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā dvai (sabbattha dvai), avigatai dvai|

anulaumaṃ|

kāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau  mauhau|

nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā dvai, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, nakammai dvai, navipākai dvai, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

6. sampayuttavārau

(aivaṃ  itarai dvai gaṇanāpi sampayuttavāraupi kātabbau|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

206. kāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kāmāvacarā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

nakāmāvacarau  dhammau nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – nakāmāvacarā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nakāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – nakāmāvacarā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) nakāmāvacarā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

207. kāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; ariyā gautrabhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti; pahīnai kilaisai…pai॰… vikkhambhitai kilaisai…pai॰… pubbai  suciṇṇāni…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ kāmāvacarai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kāmāvacarau dhammau nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, caitaupariyañāṇaina kāmāvacaracittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, kāmāvacarā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

208. nakāmāvacarau dhammau nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – nibbānaṃ maggassa, phalassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; caitaupariyañāṇaina nakāmāvacaracittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa…pai॰… nakāmāvacarā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nakāmāvacarau  dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti; phalaṃ…pai॰… nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ  pac‍cavaikkhati, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, iddhividhañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, caitaupariyañāṇaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, nakāmāvacarai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (2)

adhipatipac‍cayau

209. kāmāvacarau  dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, saikkhā gautrabhuṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ kāmāvacarai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – kāmāvacarādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

210. nakāmāvacarau dhammau nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – nibbānaṃ maggassa, phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – nakāmāvacarādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ…pai॰… nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati  viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ…pai॰… iddhividhañāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, nakāmāvacarai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – nakāmāvacarādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; nakāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa ca nakāmāvacarassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – nakāmāvacarādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

anantarapac‍cayādi

211. kāmāvacarau  dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā kāmāvacarā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kāmāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… āvaj‍janā kāmāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

kāmāvacarau dhammau nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – kāmāvacaraṃ cuticittaṃ nakāmāvacarassa upapatticittassa… āvaj‍janā nakāmāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; kāmāvacarā khandhā nakāmāvacarassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭhamassa jhānassa parikammaṃ paṭhamassa jhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… catutthassa jhānassa…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… dibbassa cakkhussa…pai॰… dibbāya sautadhātuyā…pai॰… iddhividhañāṇassa…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇassa…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇassa…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa parikammaṃ anāgataṃsañāṇassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa… anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

212. nakāmāvacarau dhammau nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā nakāmāvacarā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ nakāmāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; maggau phalassa, phalaṃ phalassa, niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – nakāmāvacaraṃ cuticittaṃ kāmāvacarassa upapatticittassa, nakāmāvacaraṃ bhavaṅgaṃ āvaj‍janāya, nakāmāvacarā khandhā kāmāvacarassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| (2)

samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cha… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

upanissayapac‍cayau

213. kāmāvacarau  dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – kāmāvacaraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; kāmāvacaraṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; kāmāvacarā saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ kāmāvacarāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya… kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kāmāvacarau dhammau nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – kāmāvacaraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya nakāmāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti; kāmāvacaraṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya nakāmāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti; kāmāvacarā saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ nakāmāvacarāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya… maggassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭhamassa jhānassa parikammaṃ paṭhamassa jhānassa…pai॰… catutthassa jhānassa…pai॰… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanassa…pai॰… paṭhamassa maggassa…pai॰… catutthassa maggassa parikammaṃ catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

214. nakāmāvacarau dhammau nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – nakāmāvacaraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti; nakāmāvacaraṃ sīlaṃ  …pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya nakāmāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti…pai॰… maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti; nakāmāvacarā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā nakāmāvacarāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya, maggassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ dutiyassa jhānassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa …pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭhamau maggau dutiyassa maggassa…pai॰… dutiyau maggau tatiyassa maggassa…pai॰… tatiyau maggau catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; maggau phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nakāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – nakāmāvacaraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; nakāmāvacaraṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; nakāmāvacarā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā kāmāvacarāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya, rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa  dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; ariyā maggaṃ upanissāya saṅkhārai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassanti, maggau ariyānaṃ atthapaṭisambhidāya… dhammapaṭisambhidāya… niruttipaṭisambhidāya… paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāya… ṭhānāṭhānakausal‍lassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; phalasamāpatti kāyikassa sukhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

puraijātapac‍cayādi

215. kāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa …pai॰…| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu kāmāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kāmāvacarau dhammau  nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu nakāmāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

kammapac‍cayādi

216. kāmāvacarau  dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nakāmāvacarau dhammau nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā (saṃkhittaṃ)| nakāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā (saṃkhittaṃ)| nakāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa ca nakāmāvacarassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā (saṃkhittaṃ)| (3)

vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

217. kāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| kāmāvacarau dhammau nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ) paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu nakāmāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu nakāmāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

nakāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – nakāmāvacarā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| pacchājātā – nakāmāvacarā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

atthipac‍cayādi

218. kāmāvacarau  dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| kāmāvacarau dhammau nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātaṃ – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu nakāmāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati…pai॰… (puraijātasadisaṃ)| (2)

nakāmāvacarau dhammau nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| nakāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (vippayuttasadisaṃ)| nakāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa ca nakāmāvacarassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (paṭic‍casadisaṃ)| (3)

219. kāmāvacarau ca nakāmāvacarau ca dhammā kāmāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – nakāmāvacarā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| pacchājātā – nakāmāvacarā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – nakāmāvacarā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kāmāvacarau ca nakāmāvacarau ca dhammā nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – nakāmāvacarau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

220. haituyā  cattāri, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā cattāri, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai satta, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nissayai satta, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai dvai, pacchājātai dvai, āsaivanai  tīṇi, kammai cattāri, vipākai cattāri, āhārai cattāri, indriyai cattāri, jhānai cattāri, maggai cattāri, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā satta, natthiyā cattāri, vigatai cattāri, avigatai satta|

2. pac‍canīyuddhārau

221. kāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kāmāvacarau dhammau nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

222. nakāmāvacarau dhammau nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakāmāvacarau dhammau kāmāvacarassa ca nakāmāvacarassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

kāmāvacarau ca nakāmāvacarau ca dhammā kāmāvacarassa  dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| kāmāvacarau ca nakāmāvacarau ca dhammā nakāmāvacarassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| (2)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

223. nahaituyā satta, naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai satta, na sahajātai cha, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nanissayai cha, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai cha…pai॰… nasampayuttai cha, navippayuttai  pañ‍ca, nauatthiyā pañ‍ca, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta, nauavigatai pañ‍ca|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

224. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cattāri, naadhipatiyā cattāri, naanantarai cattāri, nasamanantarai cattāri, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, naupanissayai cattāri…pai॰… nasampayuttai dvai, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā cattāri, nauvigatai cattāri|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

225. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā cattāri (anulaumamātikā kātabbā)…pai॰… avigatai satta|

kāmāvacaradukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

94. rūpāvacaradukaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

226. rūpāvacaraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| rūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – rūpāvacarai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| rūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau ca narūpāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

227. narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – narūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai  …pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarā khandhā| narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau ca narūpāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarā khandhā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

228. rūpāvacarañ‍ca narūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| rūpāvacarañ‍ca narūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – rūpāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai  rūpāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| rūpāvacarañ‍ca narūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau ca narūpāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… rūpāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (3)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

229. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nissayai nava, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai dvai, āsaivanai dvai, kammai nava, vipākai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai cattāri, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā cattāri, vigatai cattāri, avigatai nava|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitu-naārammaṇapac‍cayā

230. narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ narūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai  khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naadhipatipac‍cayādi

231. rūpāvacaraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā  – rūpāvacarai khandhai paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarādhipati vipākaṃ, rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| rūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākai rūpāvacarai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| rūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau ca narūpāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākaṃ rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – narūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… tīṇi| (nakāmāvacaraṃ paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ nin‍nānaṃ, idha sabbai mahābhūtā kātabbā|) (3)

232. rūpāvacarañ‍ca narūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| rūpāvacarañ‍ca narūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākai rūpāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| rūpāvacarañ‍ca narūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau ca narūpāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… rūpāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3) naanantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… naupanissayapac‍cayā|

napuraijātapac‍cayādi

233. rūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| rūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – rūpāvacarai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| rūpāvacaraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau ca  narūpāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti napuraijātapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai narūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… narūpāvacarai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā, itarai pañ‍capi pañhā, anulaumaṃ kātabbaṃ) napacchājātapac‍cayā… nava|

naāsaivanapac‍cayādi

234. rūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| rūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – rūpāvacarai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| rūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau ca narūpāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naāsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai  …pai॰…| (3)

narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

rūpāvacarañ‍ca narūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ| mūlaṃ| itaraipi pañhā kātabbā)… nakammapac‍cayā… dvai…pai॰… nasampayuttapac‍cayā|

235. narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai narūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… (saṃkhittaṃ)|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

236. nahaituyā  aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai dvai, navipākai pañ‍ca, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai aikaṃ, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

2. sahajātavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi sahajātavāraupi kātabbau|)

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

237. rūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭic‍casadisaṃ)|

narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – narūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā  tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… (yāva ajjhattikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ) vatthuṃ pac‍cayā narūpāvacarā khandhā| narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā rūpāvacarau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā rūpāvacarā khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā rūpāvacarau ca narūpāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā rūpāvacarā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

238. rūpāvacarañ‍ca narūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| rūpāvacarañ‍ca narūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – rūpāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| rūpāvacarañ‍ca narūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā rūpāvacarau ca narūpāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… rūpāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

239. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nissayai nava, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai cattāri, āsaivanai cattāri, kammai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

240. narūpāvacaraṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ  narūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā narūpāvacarā khandhā, vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

naadhipatipac‍cayau

241. rūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ)|

narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ)| narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā rūpāvacarādhipati, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā rūpāvacarā khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā rūpāvacarau ca narūpāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā rūpāvacarā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

242. rūpāvacarañ‍ca narūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – rūpāvacarai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā rūpāvacarādhipati, vipākaṃ  rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai  khandhai ca…pai॰…| rūpāvacarañ‍ca narūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākai rūpāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| rūpāvacarañ‍ca narūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā rūpāvacarau ca narūpāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākaṃ rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… vipākai rūpāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

243. nahaituyā  aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi…pai॰… naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava (suddhikai arūpai ca missakai ca ‘‘vipāka’’nti niyāmaitabbaṃ), nakammai cattāri, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai aikaṃ, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

4. nissayavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi nissayavāraupi kātabbau|)

5. saṃsaṭṭhavārau

1-4. pac‍cayānulaumādi

haitupac‍cayau

244. rūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – rūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

narūpāvacaraṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – narūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau  khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

haituyā  dvai, ārammaṇai dvai (sabbattha dvai), avigatai dvai|

anulaumaṃ|

narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā dvai, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, nakammai dvai, navipākai dvai, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai aikaṃ|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

6. sampayuttavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi sampayuttavāraupi kātabbau|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

245. rūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – rūpāvacarā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) rūpāvacarā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) rūpāvacarā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

narūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – narūpāvacarā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ  khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

246. rūpāvacarau  dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – caitaupariyañāṇaina rūpāvacaracittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, rūpāvacarā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ…pai॰… iddhividhañāṇaṃ…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, rūpāvacarai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (2)

247. narūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa, phalassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; ariyā pahīnai kilaisai…pai॰… vikkhambhitai kilaisai…pai॰… pubbai…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ narūpāvacarai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa…pai॰… rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

narūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, caitaupariyañāṇaina narūpāvacaracittasamaṅgissa  cittaṃ jānāti, narūpāvacarā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

adhipatipac‍cayau

248. rūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – rūpāvacarādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ…pai॰… iddhividhañāṇaṃ…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, rūpāvacarai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – rūpāvacarādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa ca narūpāvacarassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – rūpāvacarādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

249. narūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… pubbai  suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati; ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… phalaṃ…pai॰… nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa, phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ narūpāvacarai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati; ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ garuṃ  katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| sahajātādhipati – narūpāvacarādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anantarapac‍cayādi

250. rūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā rūpāvacarā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – rūpāvacaraṃ cuticittaṃ narūpāvacarassa upapatticittassa  anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; rūpāvacaraṃ bhavaṅgaṃ āvaj‍janāya… rūpāvacarā khandhā narūpāvacarassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

251. narūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā narūpāvacarā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ narūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| narūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – narūpāvacaraṃ cuticittaṃ rūpāvacarassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; narūpāvacarā khandhā rūpāvacarassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭhamassa jhānassa parikammaṃ paṭhamassa jhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… catutthassa jhānassa…pai॰… dibbassa cakkhussa…pai॰… dibbāya sautadhātuyā…pai॰… iddhividhañāṇassa…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇassa…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇassa…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa, parikammaṃ anāgataṃsañāṇassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cha… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

upanissayapac‍cayau

252. rūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – rūpāvacaraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya rūpāvacaraṃ  jhānaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti; rūpāvacaraṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya rūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti; rūpāvacarā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā rūpāvacarāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ dutiyassa jhānassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ tatiyassa jhānassa…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ catutthassa jhānassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

rūpāvacarau  dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – rūpāvacaraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ  karauti; rūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… vipassanaṃ…pai॰… maggaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; rūpāvacaraṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; rūpāvacarā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā narūpāvacarāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya… rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya… kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa… maggassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

253. narūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – narūpāvacaraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ karauti; narūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… vipassanaṃ…pai॰… maggaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; narūpāvacaraṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ, rāgaṃ…pai॰… bhaujanaṃ, saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; narūpāvacarā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā… rāgau…pai॰… saināsanaṃ narūpāvacarāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya… rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya… kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa… maggassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

narūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – narūpāvacaraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya rūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ  …pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti; narūpāvacaraṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ  upanissāya rūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti; narūpāvacarā saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ rūpāvacarāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, paṭhamassa jhānassa parikammaṃ paṭhamassa jhānassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… catutthassa jhānassa…pai॰… dibbassa cakkhussa parikammaṃ…pai॰… dibbāya sautadhātuyā…pai॰… iddhividhañāṇassa…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇassa…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇassa…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa parikammaṃ anāgataṃsañāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

puraijātapac‍cayādi

254. narūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ  …pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; vatthu narūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| narūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu rūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

kammapac‍cayādi

255. rūpāvacarau  dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – rūpāvacarā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – rūpāvacarā caitanā vipākānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – rūpāvacarā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – rūpāvacarā caitanā kaṭattārūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa ca narūpāvacarassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – rūpāvacarā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – rūpāvacarā caitanā vipākānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

narūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – narūpāvacarā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – narūpāvacarā caitanā vipākānaṃ narūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| (1)

vipākapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… cattāri… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… maggapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… cattāri… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

256. rūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

narūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| narūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātaṃ – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu rūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu rūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

atthipac‍cayādi

257. rūpāvacarau  dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (paṭic‍casadisaṃ)| rūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| rūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa ca narūpāvacarassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (paṭic‍casadisaṃ)| (3)

narūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| narūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātaṃ – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu rūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, vatthu rūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

258. rūpāvacarau ca narūpāvacarau ca dhammā rūpāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – rūpāvacarau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| rūpāvacarau ca narūpāvacarau ca dhammā narūpāvacarassa  dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – rūpāvacarā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| pacchājātā – rūpāvacarā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – rūpāvacarā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

259. haituyā  cattāri, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā cattāri, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai satta, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nissayai satta, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai dvai, pacchājātai dvai, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai cattāri, vipākai cattāri, āhārai cattāri, indriyai cattāri, jhānai cattāri, maggai cattāri, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā satta, natthiyā cattāri, vigatai cattāri, avigatai satta|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

260. rūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| rūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa  dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa ca narūpāvacarassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

261. narūpāvacarau dhammau narūpāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| narūpāvacarau dhammau rūpāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

rūpāvacarau ca narūpāvacarau ca dhammā rūpāvacarassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| rūpāvacarau  ca narūpāvacarau ca dhammā narūpāvacarassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (2)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

262. nahaituyā satta, naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai satta, nasahajātai cha, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nanissayai cha, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai satta…pai॰… nasampayuttai cha, navippayuttai pañ‍ca, nauatthiyā pañ‍ca, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta, nauavigatai pañ‍ca|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

263. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai cattāri, naadhipatiyā cattāri, naanantarai nasamanantarai cattāri, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, naupanissayai cattāri…pai॰… nasampayuttai dvai, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā cattāri, nauvigatai cattāri|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

264. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā cattāri (anulaumamātikā kātabbā)…pai॰… avigatai satta|

rūpāvacaradukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

95. arūpāvacaradukaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

265. arūpāvacaraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca arūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – arūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| arūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – arūpāvacarai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| arūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca arūpāvacarau ca naarūpāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – arūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

266. naarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naarūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ  …pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai naarūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

arūpāvacarañ‍ca naarūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – arūpāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

267. haituyā  pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai dvai, samanantarai dvai, sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai pañ‍ca, upanissayai dvai, puraijātai dvai, āsaivanai dvai, kammai pañ‍ca, vipākai dvai, āhārai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitu-naārammaṇapac‍cayā

268. naarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naarūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ  …pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1) naārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naadhipatipac‍cayādi

269. arūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca arūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – arūpāvacarai khandhai paṭic‍ca arūpāvacarādhipati, vipākaṃ arūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

naarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – naarūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā  cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai …pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā)| (1) naanantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… naupanissayapac‍cayā|

napuraijātapac‍cayādi

270. arūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca arūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai arūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) arūpāvacarai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

naarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai naarūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā)| (1)

arūpāvacarañ‍ca naarūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1) napacchājātapac‍cayā…|

naāsaivanapac‍cayau

271. arūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca arūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ arūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| arūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – arūpāvacarai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

naarūpāvacaraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – naarūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā)| (1)

arūpāvacarañ‍ca naarūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā  – arūpāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai  ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

272. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā dvai, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai cattāri, napacchājātai pañ‍ca, naāsaivanai cattāri, nakammai dvai, navipākai pañ‍ca, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

2. sahajātavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi sahajātavāraupi kātabbau|)

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

273. arūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā arūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭic‍casadisā)|

naarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naarūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰… (yāva ajjhattikā mahābhūtā) vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naarūpāvacarā khandhā| naarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā arūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā arūpāvacarā khandhā| naarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā arūpāvacarau ca  naarūpāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā arūpāvacarā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

274. arūpāvacarañ‍ca  naarūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ  pac‍cayā arūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – arūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| arūpāvacarañ‍ca naarūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – arūpāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| arūpāvacarañ‍ca naarūpāvacarañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā arūpāvacarau ca naarūpāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – arūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… arūpāvacarai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

275. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cattāri, nissayai nava, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai cattāri, āsaivanai cattāri, kammai nava, vipākai dvai…pai॰… avigatai nava|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayādi

276. naarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naarūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā naarūpāvacarā khandhā, vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1) naārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

arūpāvacaraṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā arūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – arūpāvacarai khandhai pac‍cayā arūpāvacarādhipati, vipākaṃ arūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

naarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā  naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – naarūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naarūpāvacarā khandhā| (1)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

277. nahaituyā  aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā cattāri, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai cattāri, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai cattāri, nakammai cattāri, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

4. nissayavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi nissayavāraupi kātabbau|)

5. saṃsaṭṭhavārau

1-4. pac‍cayānulaumādi

haitupac‍cayau

278. arūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau arūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – arūpāvacaraṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

naarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naarūpāvacaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

haituyā dvai…pai॰… avigatai dvai (anulaumaṃ)|

nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā dvai, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, nakammai dvai, navipākai dvai, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai (pac‍canīyaṃ)|

6. sampayuttavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi sampayuttavāraupi kātabbau|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

279. arūpāvacarau  dhammau arūpāvacarassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – arūpāvacarā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) arūpāvacarā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) arūpāvacarā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naarūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naarūpāvacarā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

280. arūpāvacarau dhammau arūpāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| arūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, arūpāvacarai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; caitaupariyañāṇaina arūpāvacaracittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, arūpāvacarā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naarūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa  dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa, phalassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; ariyā pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai…pai॰… pubbai…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ naarūpāvacarai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, caitaupariyañāṇaina naarūpāvacaracittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ  kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… naarūpāvacarā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

281. arūpāvacarau dhammau arūpāvacarassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – arūpāvacarādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| arūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, arūpāvacarai  khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – arūpāvacarādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) arūpāvacarādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

282. naarūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati; pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ…pai॰… nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa, phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ naarūpāvacarai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – naarūpāvacarādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anantarapac‍cayādi

283. arūpāvacarau  dhammau arūpāvacarassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā  purimā arūpāvacarā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| arūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – arūpāvacaraṃ cuticittaṃ naarūpāvacarassa upapatticittassa, arūpāvacaraṃ bhavaṅgaṃ  āvaj‍janāya, arūpāvacarā khandhā naarūpāvacarassa vuṭṭhānassa, niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

284. naarūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā naarūpāvacarā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ naarūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa…pai॰… anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naarūpāvacarau dhammau arūpāvacarassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naarūpāvacaraṃ cuticittaṃ arūpāvacarassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; naarūpāvacarā khandhā arūpāvacarassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; ākāsānañ‍cāyatanassa parikammaṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanassa…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

upanissayapac‍cayau

285. arūpāvacarau dhammau arūpāvacarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanassa…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ  naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| arūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau  – arūpāvacaraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ karauti, naarūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; arūpāvacaraṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti… mānaṃ jappaiti… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; arūpāvacarā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā naarūpāvacarāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya… rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya… kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa… maggassa phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

286. naarūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – naarūpāvacaraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ karauti, naarūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; naarūpāvacaraṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… rāgaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ…pai॰… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti… pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; naarūpāvacarā saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ naarūpāvacarāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya… kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa, maggassa, phalasamāpattiyā  upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naarūpāvacarau dhammau arūpāvacarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – ākāsānañ‍cāyatanassa parikammaṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

puraijātapac‍cayādi

287. naarūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰…| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ  cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu naarūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naarūpāvacarau dhammau arūpāvacarassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu arūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

kammapac‍cayau

288. arūpāvacarau dhammau arūpāvacarassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā…pai॰…| arūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – arūpāvacarā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) arūpāvacarā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naarūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – naarūpāvacarā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

vipākapac‍cayādi

289. arūpāvacarau dhammau arūpāvacarassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naarūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

290. arūpāvacarau  dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

naarūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| naarūpāvacarau dhammau  arūpāvacarassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu arūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

atthipac‍cayādi

291. arūpāvacarau dhammau arūpāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ…pai॰…| arūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ…pai॰…| arūpāvacarau dhammau arūpāvacarassa ca naarūpāvacarassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (3)

naarūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| naarūpāvacarau dhammau arūpāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu arūpāvacarānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

292. arūpāvacarau ca naarūpāvacarau ca dhammā arūpāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – arūpāvacarau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca…pai॰…| arūpāvacarau ca naarūpāvacarau ca dhammā naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – arūpāvacarā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – arūpāvacarā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau  āhārau ca imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – arūpāvacarā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

293. haituyā cattāri, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā cattāri, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai satta, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai dvai, pacchājātai dvai, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai cattāri, vipākai dvai, āhārai cattāri, indriyai  cattāri, jhānai cattāri, maggai cattāri, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā satta, natthiyā cattāri, vigatai cattāri, avigatai satta|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

294. arūpāvacarau dhammau arūpāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| arūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| arūpāvacarau dhammau arūpāvacarassa ca naarūpāvacarassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

295. naarūpāvacarau dhammau naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naarūpāvacarau dhammau arūpāvacarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

arūpāvacarau  ca naarūpāvacarau ca dhammā arūpāvacarassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| arūpāvacarau ca naarūpāvacarau ca dhammā naarūpāvacarassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (2)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

296. nahaituyā satta, naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai satta, nasahajātai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, nanissayai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai cha, napacchājātai satta…pai॰… nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai cattāri, nauatthiyā cattāri, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta, nauavigatai cattāri|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

297. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai cattāri, naadhipatiyā cattāri, naanantarai nasamanantarai cattāri, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, naupanissayai cattāri…pai॰… nasampayuttai dvai, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā cattāri, nauvigatai cattāri|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

298. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā cattāri (anulaumamātikā kātabbā)…pai॰… avigatai satta|

arūpāvacaradukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

96. pariyāpan‍nadukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

299. pariyāpan‍naṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pariyāpan‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – pariyāpan‍naṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (yathā  cūḷantaradukai laukiyadukaṃ| aivaṃ imampi dukaṃ kātabbaṃ, nin‍nānākaraṇaṃ|)

pariyāpan‍nadukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

97. niyyānikadukaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

300. niyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca niyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – niyyānikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| niyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – niyyānikai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| niyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca niyyānikau ca aniyyānikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – niyyānikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

aniyyānikaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – aniyyānikaṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

niyyānikañ‍ca aniyyānikañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – niyyānikai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

301. haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai  samanantarai dvai, sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai pañ‍ca, upanissayai dvai, puraijātai dvai, āsaivanai dvai, kammai pañ‍ca, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitu-naārammaṇapac‍cayā

302. aniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ aniyyānikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1) naārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naadhipatipac‍cayādi

303. niyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca niyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – niyyānikai khandhai paṭic‍ca niyyānikādhipati| (1)

aniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – aniyyānikaṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā kātabbā)| (1)

naanantarapac‍cayā… nasamanantarapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā…pai॰…|

napuraijātapac‍cayau

304. niyyānikaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca niyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai niyyānikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰…| niyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – niyyānikai  khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

aniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai aniyyānikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… aniyyānikai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā)| (1)

niyyānikañ‍ca aniyyānikañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – niyyānikai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

305. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā dvai, naanantarai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai cattāri, napacchājātai pañ‍ca, naāsaivanai aikaṃ, nakammai dvai, navipākai pañ‍ca, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|
