haitupac‍cayau

306. niyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā niyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭic‍casadisā)|

aniyyānikaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – aniyyānikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… (yāva ajjhattikā mahābhūtā) vatthuṃ pac‍cayā aniyyānikā khandhā| aniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā niyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā  niyyānikā khandhā| aniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā niyyānikau ca aniyyānikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā niyyānikā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

307. niyyānikañ‍ca aniyyānikañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā niyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – niyyānikaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| niyyānikañ‍ca aniyyānikañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – niyyānikai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| niyyānikañ‍ca aniyyānikañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā niyyānikau ca aniyyānikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā  – niyyānikaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… niyyānikai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

308. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cattāri, nissayai nava, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai cattāri, āsaivanai cattāri, kammai nava, vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai nava|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

309. aniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ aniyyānikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā  cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā aniyyānikā khandhā, vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayādi

310. niyyānikaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

niyyānikaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā niyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – niyyānikai khandhai pac‍cayā niyyānikādhipati| (1)

aniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – aniyyānikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā aniyyānikā khandhā| aniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā niyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā niyyānikādhipati| (2)

niyyānikañ‍ca aniyyānikañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā niyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – niyyānikai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā niyyānikādhipati| (1)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

311. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā cattāri, naanantarai tīṇi…pai॰… naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai cattāri, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai aikaṃ, nakammai cattāri, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

4. nissayavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi nissayavāraupi kātabbau|)

5. saṃsaṭṭhavārau

1-4. pac‍cayānulaumādi

312. niyyānikaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau niyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – niyyānikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

aniyyānikaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – aniyyānikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

haituyā dvai, ārammaṇai dvai (sabbattha dvai), vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai dvai (anulaumaṃ)|

nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā dvai, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai aikaṃ, nakammai dvai, navipākai dvai, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai (pac‍canīyaṃ)|

6. sampayuttavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi sampayuttavāraupi kātabbau|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

313. niyyānikau dhammau niyyānikassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – niyyānikā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) niyyānikā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) niyyānikā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

aniyyānikau dhammau aniyyānikassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – aniyyānikā haitū  sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

314. niyyānikau dhammau aniyyānikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, caitaupariyañāṇaina niyyānikacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, niyyānikā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyyānikau  dhammau aniyyānikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… ariyā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, phalassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; ariyā pahīnai kilaisai…pai॰… vikkhambhitai…pai॰… pubbai samudāciṇṇai…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ aniyyānikai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, caitaupariyañāṇaina aniyyānikacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa…pai॰… rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… aniyyānikā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

aniyyānikau  dhammau niyyānikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – nibbānaṃ maggassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

adhipatipac‍cayau

315. niyyānikau  dhammau niyyānikassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – niyyānikādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niyyānikau dhammau aniyyānikassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| sahajātādhipati – niyyānikādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) niyyānikādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

316. aniyyānikau dhammau aniyyānikassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… ariyā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ aniyyānikai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – aniyyānikādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ  adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyyānikau dhammau niyyānikassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – nibbānaṃ maggassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

anantarapac‍cayādi

317. niyyānikau dhammau aniyyānikassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – maggau phalassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyyānikau dhammau aniyyānikassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā aniyyānikā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ aniyyānikānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… phalaṃ phalassa… anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā … niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyyānikau dhammau niyyānikassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… gautrabhu maggassa; vaudānaṃ maggassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

upanissayapac‍cayau

318. niyyānikau dhammau niyyānikassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pakatūpanissayau – paṭhamau maggau dutiyassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… tatiyau maggau catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niyyānikau dhammau aniyyānikassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – ariyā maggaṃ upanissāya anuppan‍naṃ samāpattiṃ uppādainti, uppan‍naṃ samāpaj‍janti, saṅkhārai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassanti, maggau ariyānaṃ atthapaṭisambhidāya…pai॰… paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāya ṭhānāṭhānakausal‍lassa  upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, maggau phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

319. aniyyānikau dhammau aniyyānikassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – aniyyānikaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ uppādaiti… vipassanaṃ… abhiñ‍ñaṃ… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti… mānaṃ jappaiti… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; aniyyānikaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; aniyyānikā saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ aniyyānikāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya… kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyyānikau dhammau niyyānikassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – paṭhamassa maggassa parikammaṃ paṭhamassa  maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… catutthassa maggassa parikammaṃ catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

puraijātapac‍cayādi

320. aniyyānikau  dhammau aniyyānikassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai (arūpadukasadisā kātabbā)… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

kammapac‍cayau

321. niyyānikau dhammau niyyānikassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – niyyānikā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niyyānikau dhammau aniyyānikassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – niyyānikā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – niyyānikā caitanā phalassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ) niyyānikā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

322. aniyyānikau  dhammau aniyyānikassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā (saṃkhittaṃ)| nānākkhaṇikā – aniyyānikā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… aikaṃyaiva… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (arūpadukasadisā kātabbā)… atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta (arūpadukasadisā kātabbā, āmasanā nānāpadāyaiva)… natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

323. haituyā  cattāri, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai satta, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai dvai, pacchājātai dvai, āsaivanai dvai, kammai cattāri, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai cattāri, indriyai cattāri, jhānai cattāri, maggai cattāri, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā satta, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai satta|

anulaumaṃ|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

324. niyyānikau dhammau niyyānikassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niyyānikau dhammau aniyyānikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niyyānikau dhammau niyyānikassa ca aniyyānikassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

325. aniyyānikau dhammau aniyyānikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyyānikau dhammau niyyānikassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

niyyānikau ca aniyyānikau ca dhammā niyyānikassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| niyyānikau  ca aniyyānikau ca dhammā aniyyānikassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (3)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

326. nahaituyā  satta, naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai nasamanantarai satta, nasahajātai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, nanissayai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai cha, napacchājātai satta…pai॰… nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai cattāri, nauatthiyā cattāri, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta, nauavigatai cattāri|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

327. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cattāri, naadhipatiyā cattāri, naanantarai nasamanantarai cattāri, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, naupanissayai cattāri …pai॰… nasampayuttai dvai, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā cattāri, nauvigatai cattāri|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

328. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca (anulaumamātikā vitthāraitabbā)…pai॰… avigatai satta|

niyyānikadukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

98. niyatadukaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

329. niyataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca niyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  – niyataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| niyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā – niyatai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ| pañ‍capi pañhā kātabbā| yathā niyyānikadukaṃ aivaṃ paṭic‍cavāraupi sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi kātabbā, nin‍nānākaraṇaṃ āmasanaṃ nānaṃ)|

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

330. niyatau dhammau niyatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri (niyyānikadukasadisā nin‍nānākaraṇā)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

331. niyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, niyatai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai…pai॰… niyatai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, caitaupariyañāṇaina niyatacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, niyatā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha aniyatau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ…pai॰… aniyataṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, ariyā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ  pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, phalassa, āvaj‍janāya  ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; ariyā aniyatai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai…pai॰… pubbai samudāciṇṇai…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ aniyatai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha aniyatau rāgau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati…pai॰… āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatau dhammau niyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; nibbānaṃ maggassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; rūpajīvitindriyaṃ mātughātikammassa… pitughātikammassa… arahantaghātikammassa… ruhiruppādakammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; yaṃ vatthuṃ āmasantassa micchattaniyatā khandhā uppaj‍janti, taṃ vatthu micchattaniyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

adhipatipac‍cayau

332. niyatau dhammau niyatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – niyatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| sahajātādhipati – niyatādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niyatau dhammau niyatassa ca aniyatassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – niyatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ  khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

333. aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā aniyatau rāgau uppaj‍jati… diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati; pubbai…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… ariyā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ aniyatai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā aniyatau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – aniyatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca  rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| aniyatau dhammau niyatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – nibbānaṃ maggassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

anantarapac‍cayādi

334. niyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – maggau phalassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, niyatā khandhā vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā aniyatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ aniyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… phalaṃ phalassa… anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ phalasamāpattiyā  anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatau dhammau niyatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – aniyataṃ daumanassaṃ niyatassa daumanassassa, aniyatā micchādiṭṭhi niyatamicchādiṭṭhiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

upanissayapac‍cayau

335. niyatau dhammau niyatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pakatūpanissayau – mātughātikammaṃ mātughātikammassa… pitughātikammassa… arahantaghātikammassa… ruhiruppādakammassa… saṅghabhaidakammassa, niyatamicchādiṭṭhiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (cak‍kaṃ)| paṭhamau maggau dutiyassa maggassa…pai॰… tatiyau maggau catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaitvā…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhinditvā tassa paṭighātatthāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ  karauti, ariyā  maggaṃ upanissāya anuppan‍naṃ samāpattiṃ uppādainti, uppan‍naṃ samāpaj‍janti…pai॰… ṭhānāṭhānakausal‍lassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; maggau phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

336. aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – aniyataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; aniyataṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti, aniyatā saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ aniyatāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya… kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau dhammau niyatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – aniyataṃ rāgaṃ upanissāya mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiti…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; aniyataṃ dausaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiti…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; aniyatau rāgau… dausaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ mātughātikammassa…pai॰… saṅghabhaidakammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭhamassa maggassa parikammaṃ paṭhamassa maggassa…pai॰… catutthassa maggassa parikammaṃ catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

puraijātapac‍cayādi

337. aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| aniyatau dhammau niyatassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – rūpajīvitindriyaṃ mātughātikammassa… pitughātikammassa… arahantaghātikammassa… ruhiruppādakammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu niyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

kammapac‍cayau

338. niyatau  dhammau niyatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – niyatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – niyatā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – niyatā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) niyatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… aikaṃ… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (arūpadukasadisaṃ)… atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta (arūpāvacaradukasadisaṃ)… natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

339. haituyā  cattāri, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai satta, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai dvai, pacchājātai dvai, āsaivanai dvai, kammai cattāri, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai cattāri, indriyai jhānai cattāri, maggai cattāri, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā satta, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai satta|

2. pac‍canīyuddhārau

340. niyatau  dhammau niyatassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niyatau dhammau niyatassa ca aniyatassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

341. aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatau dhammau niyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

niyatau  ca aniyatau ca dhammā niyatassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| niyatau ca aniyatau ca dhammā aniyatassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (2)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

342. nahaituyā satta, naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai nasamanantarai satta, nasahajātai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, nanissayai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai cha, napacchājātai satta…pai॰… nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai cattāri, nauatthiyā cattāri, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta, nauavigatai cattāri|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

343. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cattāri, naadhipatiyā cattāri, naanantarai nasamanantarai cattāri, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, naupanissayai cattāri…pai॰… nasampayuttai dvai, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā cattāri, nauvigatai cattāri|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

344. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca (anulaumamātikā vitthāraitabbā)…pai॰… avigatai satta|

niyatadukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

99. sauttaradukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

345. sauttaraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sauttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – sauttaraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (yāva ajjhattikā mahābhūtā| yathā cūḷantaradukai laukiyadukasadisaṃ nin‍nānākaraṇaṃ|)

sauttaradukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

100. saraṇadukaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1-4. pac‍cayānulaumādi

haitupac‍cayau

346. saraṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – saraṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (pañ‍ca pañhā arūpāvacaradukasadisā, anulaumapaṭic‍casadisā|)

haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca|

anulaumaṃ|

saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā  – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

araṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ araṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā)| (1)

nahaituyā dvai, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā pañ‍ca, naanantarai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai cattāri, napacchājātai pañ‍ca, naāsaivanai pañ‍ca, nakammai dvai, navipākai pañ‍ca, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai  aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

2. sahajātavārau

(aivaṃ  itarai dvai gaṇanāpi sahajātavāraupi kātabbau|)

3. pac‍cayavārau

1-4. pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

347. saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – saraṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… (yathā arūpāvacaradukassa pac‍cayavāraupi aivaṃ kātabbau)|

haituyā nava, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava|

anulaumaṃ|

348. saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau  uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

araṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ araṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā) cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā khandhā| araṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (2)

saraṇañ‍ca araṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai  uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

nahaituyā  cattāri, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai cattāri, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai cattāri, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

4-5. nissaya-saṃsaṭṭhavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi nissayavāraupi kātabbau, saṃsaṭṭhavāraipi dvai pañhā kātabbā sabbattha|)

haituyā dvai, ārammaṇai dvai (sabbattha dvai), vipākai aikaṃ, avigatai dvai (anulaumaṃ)|

nahaituyā dvai, naadhipatiyā dvai, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai  dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, nakammai dvai, navipākai dvai, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai (pac‍canīyaṃ)|

6. sampayuttavārau

(aivaṃ itarai dvai gaṇanāpi sampayuttavāraupi kātabbau|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

349. saraṇau  dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (arūpadukasadisaṃ, cattāri)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

350. saraṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – rāgaṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhiṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, vicikicchaṃ ārabbha  …pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ ārabbha…pai॰… daumanassaṃ ārabbha daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| saraṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā pahīnai kilaisai…pai॰… vikkhambhitai kilaisai…pai॰… pubbai samudāciṇṇai…pai॰… saraṇai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, caitaupariyañāṇaina saraṇacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| saraṇā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

351. araṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ…pai॰… nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti; nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa, phalassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ araṇai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati …pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| araṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… pubbai…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ, araṇai khandhai assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (2)

adhipatipac‍cayau

352. saraṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – rāgaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati… diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati; diṭṭhiṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati| sahajātādhipati – saraṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saraṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – saraṇādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saraṇau dhammau saraṇassa ca araṇassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – saraṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

353. araṇau  dhammau araṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ  …pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ…pai॰… nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa, phalassa, adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – araṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| araṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ…pai॰… sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati… diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati; pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ, araṇai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati… diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

anantarapac‍cayādi

354. saraṇau  dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā saraṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ saraṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saraṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saraṇā khandhā vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

355. araṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā araṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ araṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| araṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – āvaj‍janā saraṇānaṃ  khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

upanissayapac‍cayau

356. saraṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau  – rāgaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; dausaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati, rāgau…pai॰… patthanā rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saraṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – rāgaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti; dausaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, rāgau…pai॰… patthanā saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya… kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa… maggassa… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

357. araṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti; sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti; saddhā  …pai॰… saināsanaṃ saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya… kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa… maggassa… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

araṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; sīlaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati; saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

puraijātapac‍cayādi

358. araṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati…pai॰… dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰…| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa  …pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu araṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| araṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu saraṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

kammapac‍cayau

359. saraṇau  dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saraṇā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā  – saraṇā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – saraṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (mūlaṃ kātabbaṃ|) saraṇā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

araṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… aikaṃ… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai… vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (arūpadukasadisā)|

atthipac‍cayau

360. saraṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)| saraṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| saraṇau dhammau saraṇassa ca araṇassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)| (3)

361. araṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| araṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ  …pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ  ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, vatthu saraṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saraṇau ca araṇau ca dhammā saraṇassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| saraṇau ca araṇau ca dhammā araṇassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – saraṇā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – saraṇā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau  ca imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – saraṇā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

362. haituyā cattāri, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai satta, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai dvai, pacchājātai dvai, āsaivanai dvai, kammai cattāri, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai cattāri, indriyai cattāri, jhānai cattāri, maggai cattāri, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā satta, natthiyā cattāri, vigatai cattāri, avigatai satta|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

363. saraṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saraṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saraṇau dhammau saraṇassa ca araṇassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

364. araṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| araṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saraṇau  ca araṇau ca dhammā saraṇassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| saraṇau ca araṇau ca dhammā araṇassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (2)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

365. nahaituyā satta, nasamanantarai satta, nasahajātai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, nanissayai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai cha, napacchājātai satta…pai॰… nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai cattāri, nauatthiyā cattāri, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta, nauavigatai cattāri|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

366. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cattāri, naadhipatiyā cattāri…pai॰… nasamanantarai cattāri, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, naupanissayai cattāri…pai॰… nasampayuttai dvai, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā cattāri, nauvigatai cattāri|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

367. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai cattāri (anulaumamātikā gahaitabbā)…pai॰… avigatai satta|

saraṇadukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

piṭṭhidukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammānulaumai dukapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammānulaumai dukatikapaṭṭhānaṃ

1-1 haituduka-kusalattikaṃ

1. kusalapadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

1. haituṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kusalau ca nahaitu kusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

2. nahaituṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kusalau ca nahaitu kusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

3. haituṃ kusalañ‍ca nahaituṃ kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ kusalañ‍ca nahaituṃ kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ kusalañ‍ca nahaituṃ kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kusalau ca nahaitu kusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

4. haituṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

5. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai nava, āhārai nava, indriyai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava (anulaumaṃ)|

naadhipatipac‍cayau

6. haituṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā| haituṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā| haituṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kusalau ca nahaitu kusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā| (3)

7. nahaituṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā… tīṇi|

haituṃ kusalañ‍ca nahaituṃ kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā… tīṇi|

napuraijātapac‍cayādi

8. haituṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā… nava… napacchājātapac‍cayā… nava… naāsaivanapac‍cayā … nava|

nakammapac‍cayau

9. haituṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ  kusalañ‍ca nahaituṃ kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā| (1)

navipākapac‍cayādi

10. haituṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā… nava…pai॰… navippayuttapac‍cayā… nava|

11. naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, navippayuttai nava (pac‍canīyaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ, anulauma pac‍canīyaṃ)|

naadhipatipac‍cayā haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

12. haitu  kusalau dhammau haitussa kusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu kusalau dhammau nahaitussa kusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu kusalau dhammau haitussa kusalassa ca nahaitussa kusalassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

13. haitu kusalau dhammau haitussa kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu kusalau dhammau nahaitussa kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu  kusalau ca nahaitu kusalau ca dhammā haitussa kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

14. haitu kusalau dhammau haitussa kusalassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

nahaitu kusalau dhammau nahaitussa kusalassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

haitu kusalau ca nahaitu kusalau ca dhammā haitussa kusalassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati… tīṇi|

anantarapac‍cayādi

15. haitu  kusalau dhammau haitussa kusalassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

16. haitu kusalau dhammau haitussa kusalassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu kusalau dhammau nahaitussa kusalassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau… tīṇi|

haitu kusalau ca nahaitu kusalau ca dhammā haitussa kusalassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau… tīṇi, āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

17. nahaitu kusalau dhammau nahaitussa kusalassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu kusalau dhammau haitussa kusalassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu kusalau dhammau haitussa kusalassa ca nahaitussa kusalassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

āhārapac‍cayādi

18. nahaitu  kusalau dhammau nahaitussa kusalassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

19. haitu kusalau dhammau haitussa kusalassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

20. nahaitu kusalau dhammau nahaitussa kusalassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

21. haitu  kusalau dhammau haitussa kusalassa dhammassa maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

22. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

23. haitu kusalau dhammau haitussa kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

24. nahaitu kusalau dhammau nahaitussa kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu kusalau dhammau haitussa kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu kusalau dhammau haitussa kusalassa ca nahaitussa kusalassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

25. haitu  kusalau ca nahaitu kusalau ca dhammā haitussa kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| (3)

26. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava, naadhipatiyā nava…pai॰… nauavigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ, anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

2. akusalapadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

27. haituṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu akusalau ca nahaitu akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

28. nahaituṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu akusalau ca nahaitu akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

29. haituṃ  akusalañ‍ca nahaituṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ akusalañ‍ca nahaituṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ akusalañ‍ca nahaituṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu akusalau ca nahaitu akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

30. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… kammai nava, āhārai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ, anulaumaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayau

31. nahaituṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayādi

32. haituṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā… nava…pai॰…|

haituṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ akusalañ‍ca nahaituṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

33. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, navippayuttai nava (pac‍canīyaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ, anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

34. haitu akusalau dhammau haitussa akusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu akusalau  dhammau nahaitussa akusalassa  dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu akusalau dhammau haitussa akusalassa ca nahaitussa akusalassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

35. haitu akusalau dhammau haitussa akusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu akusalau dhammau nahaitussa  akusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu akusalau ca nahaitu akusalau ca dhammā haitussa akusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

36. haitu akusalau dhammau haitussa akusalassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati… tīṇi|

nahaitu akusalau dhammau nahaitussa akusalassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

haitu akusalau ca nahaitu akusalau ca dhammā haitussa akusalassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati… tīṇi…pai॰…|

37. nahaitu akusalau dhammau nahaitussa akusalassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu akusalau dhammau haitussa akusalassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu akusalau dhammau haitussa akusalassa ca nahaitussa akusalassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

āhārapac‍cayādi

38. nahaitu akusalau dhammau nahaitussa akusalassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

39. nahaitu  akusalau dhammau nahaitussa akusalassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

40. nahaitu  akusalau dhammau nahaitussa akusalassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

41. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

42. haitu akusalau dhammau haitussa akusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

43. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai nava, nasamanantarai nava, nasahajātai nava, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava…pai॰… nauavigatai nava (pac‍canīyaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ, anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

3. abyākatapadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayādi

44. haituṃ  abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  haitu abyākatau ca nahaitu abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

45. nahaituṃ  abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

haituṃ abyākatañ‍ca nahaituṃ abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

46. haituṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

47. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai nava, vipākai nava, āhārai nava, indriyai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava (anulaumaṃ)|

nahaitu-naārammaṇapac‍cayā

48. nahaituṃ  abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

49. haituṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ abyākatañ‍ca nahaituṃ abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayādi

50. haituṃ  abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā… nava…pai॰…|

51. haituṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ  abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ abyākatañ‍ca nahaituṃ abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā| (1)

52. nahaituṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāhārapac‍cayā… naindriyapac‍cayā… najhānapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

53. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai  tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai nava, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (pac‍canīyaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ, anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

54. haitu  abyākatau dhammau haitussa abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu abyākatau dhammau nahaitussa abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu abyākatau dhammau haitussa abyākatassa ca nahaitussa abyākatassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

55. haitu  abyākatau dhammau haitussa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

56. haitu abyākatau dhammau haitussa abyākatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

nahaitu abyākatau dhammau nahaitussa abyākatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

haitu  abyākatau ca nahaitu abyākatau ca dhammā haitussa abyākatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati… tīṇi…pai॰…|

puraijātapac‍cayādi

57. nahaitu abyākatau dhammau nahaitussa abyākatassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

58. haitu abyākatau dhammau nahaitussa abyākatassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nahaitu abyākatau dhammau nahaitussa abyākatassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

haitu abyākatau ca nahaitu abyākatau ca dhammā nahaitussa abyākatassa dhammassa  pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

59. nahaitu abyākatau dhammau nahaitussa abyākatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

60. haitu abyākatau dhammau haitussa abyākatassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

61. nahaitu abyākatau dhammau nahaitussa abyākatassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

62. haitu  abyākatau dhammau nahaitussa abyākatassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nahaitu  abyākatau dhammau nahaitussa abyākatassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu abyākatau ca nahaitu abyākatau ca dhammā nahaitussa abyākatassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

63. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

64. haitu  abyākatau dhammau haitussa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu abyākatau dhammau nahaitussa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu abyākatau dhammau haitussa abyākatassa ca nahaitussa abyākatassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

65. nahaitu abyākatau dhammau nahaitussa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

66. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava, naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ, anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ)|

(yathā  kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

haitudukakusalattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

1-2. haituduka-vaidanāttikaṃ

1. sukhāyavaidanāyasampayuttapadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

67. haituṃ  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

68. nahaituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

69. haituṃ  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca nahaituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca nahaituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca  nahaituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

70. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai nava, vipākai nava, āhārai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (anulaumaṃ)|

nahaitu-naadhipatipac‍cayā

71. nahaituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

72. haituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā| haituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

73. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai nava (pac‍canīyaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ, anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

74. haitu  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

75. haitu  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nahaitussa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā haitussa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

76. haitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nahaitussa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

haitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā haitussa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati… tīṇi…pai॰…|

upanissayapac‍cayādi

77. haitu  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau… nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

78. nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nahaitussa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa ca nahaitussa  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

…vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

79. nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nahaitussa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi…pai॰…| haitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

80. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

81. haitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

82. nahaituyā  nava, naārammaṇai nava…pai॰… nauavigatai nava (pac‍canīyaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ, anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

2. dukkhāyavaidanāyasampayuttapadaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

83. haituṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya  sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nahaitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nahaituṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

haituṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca nahaituṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

84. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai  nava, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai nava, indriyai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava (anulaumaṃ)|

nahaitu-naadhipatipac‍cayā

85. nahaituṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā (1)

86. haituṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

87. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ (pac‍canīyaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ, anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

88. haitu  dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nahaitussa dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau  haitussa  dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa ca nahaitussa dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

89. haitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nahaitussa dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nahaitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā haitussa dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

90. nahaitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nahaitussa dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

91. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava (anulaumaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

92. haitu  dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

93. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava, naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ, anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

3. adukkhamasukhāyavaidanāyasampayuttapadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

94. haituṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nahaituṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

haituṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca nahaituṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

95. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai nava, vipākai nava, āhārai nava, indriyai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava (anulaumaṃ)|

nahaitu-naadhipatipac‍cayā

96. nahaituṃ  adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu  adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā… dvai|

97. haituṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

98. nahaituyā dvai, naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai nava (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ, anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayādi

99. haitu adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

100. haitu adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

101. haitu  adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

nahaitu adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nahaitussa adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

haitu adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nahaitu adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā haitussa adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati… tīṇi (ārammaṇādhipatiyaiva)…pai॰…|

upanissayapac‍cayādi

102. haitu adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau… nava, āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

103. nahaitu adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nahaitussa adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

104. haitu adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

105. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai  tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava (anulaumaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

106. haitu  adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau haitussa adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

107. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ, anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

haitudukavaidanāttikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

nahaituṃ vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

112. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai nava (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyaṃ)|

115. haitu vipākau dhammau haitussa vipākassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (sahajātādhipatiyaiva labbhati, ārammaṇādhipati natthi)…pai॰…|

upanissayapac‍cayādi

116. haitu vipākau dhammau haitussa vipākassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau| haitu vipākau dhammau nahaitussa vipākassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau| haitu vipākau dhammau haitussa vipākassa ca nahaitussa vipākassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau| (3)

117. nahaitu  vipākau dhammau nahaitussa vipākassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (sahajātakammamaiva, saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

143. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai nava, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ, pac‍canīyaṃ)|

…āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

148. nahaitu  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitussa naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

149. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā cha, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai  nava, kammai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

150. haitu naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau haitussa naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

151. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava, naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā  kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

haitudukavipākattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

1-4. haituduka-upādin‍nattikaṃ

1. upādin‍nupādāniyapadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

152. haituṃ  upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nahaituṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

haituṃ upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca nahaituṃ upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

153. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava…pai॰… kammai nava, vipākai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayādi

154. nahaituṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

155. haituṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca nahaituṃ upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

156. haituṃ  upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā… nava…pai॰…|

157. nahaituṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā… naāhārapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

158. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, navipākai aikaṃ, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai nava, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

159. haitu upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau haitussa upādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

160. haitu upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau haitussa upādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu  upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau nahaitussa upādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu upādin‍nupādāniyau ca nahaitu upādin‍nupādāniyau ca dhammā haitussa upādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

161. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai  nava, avigatai nava|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

162. haitu upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau haitussa upādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau nahaitussa upādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

163. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava, naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

nahaituṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā| nahaituṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā| (2)

haituṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca nahaituṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā| haituṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca nahaituṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā| (2) (saṃkhittaṃ|)
