190. haitu saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau haitussa saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau haitussa saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

haitu saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau haitussa saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati… nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

191. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

192. haitu  saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau haitussa saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa  dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

193. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

2. asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikapadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

194. haituṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nahaituṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

haituṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikañ‍ca nahaituṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

195. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayau

196. nahaitu  asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

197. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai  tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai nava, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

205. naadhipatiyā cha, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, navippayuttai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

228. nahaituṃ  avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca nahaituṃ avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā… nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

229. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai nava, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

230. haitu  avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau haitussa avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau haitussa avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

231. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā cha, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

232. haitu avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau haitussa avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

233. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava, naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

haitudukavitak‍kattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

234. haituṃ  pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nahaituṃ pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

235. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… kammai nava, vipākai nava, āhārai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitu-naadhipatipac‍cayā

236. nahaituṃ pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā|

haituṃ pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

237. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā  naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayādi

238. haitu  pītisahagatau dhammau haitussa pītisahagatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu pītisahagatau dhammau haitussa pītisahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu pītisahagatau dhammau nahaitussa pītisahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu pītisahagatau ca nahaitu pītisahagatau ca dhammā haitussa pītisahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu pītisahagatau dhammau haitussa pītisahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

nahaitu pītisahagatau dhammau nahaitussa pītisahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

haitu pītisahagatau ca nahaitu pītisahagatau ca dhammā haitussa pītisahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

239. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

272. haitu bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau haitussa bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

273. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi naṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

3. naivadassanainanabhāvanāyapahātabbapadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

274. haituṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  (saṃkhittaṃ)|

275. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… kammai nava, vipākai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayādi

276. nahaituṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā|

haituṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ  naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca nahaituṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

277. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai nava, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

haitupac‍cayādi

310. haitu  ācayagāmī dhammau haitussa ācayagāmissa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu ācayagāmī dhammau haitussa ācayagāmissa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

nahaitu ācayagāmī dhammau nahaitussa ācayagāmissa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

haitu ācayagāmī ca nahaitu ācayagāmī ca dhammā haitussa ācayagāmissa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

haitupac‍cayā  naadhipatiyā cha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatipac‍cayā haituyā cha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitu-adhipatipac‍cayā

318. haitu  apacayagāmī dhammau haitussa apacayagāmissa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu  apacayagāmī dhammau haitussa apacayagāmissa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

nahaitu apacayagāmī dhammau nahaitussa apacayagāmissa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātādhipati… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

319. haituyā tīṇi, adhipatiyā cha, sahajātai nava…pai॰… upanissayai nava, kammai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

320. haitu apacayagāmī dhammau haitussa apacayagāmissa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

321. nahaituyā  nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā adhipatiyā tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi naṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

3. naivācayagāmināpacayagāmipadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

322. haituṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

haituṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca nahaituṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

323. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… upanissayai nava, puraijātai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai nava, vipākai nava, āhārai nava, indriyai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayādi

324. nahaituṃ  naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca nahaituṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰…|

haituṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

haituṃ  naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati napacchājātapac‍cayā…pai॰…|

haituṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

325. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai  aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai nava, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

326. haitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau haitussa naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau haitussa naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

327. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

328. haitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau haitussa naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa dhammassa  ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau nahaitussa naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

329. nahaituyā  nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi naṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

348. nahaituṃ naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (2)

haituṃ naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau  dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ naivasaikkhanāsaikkhañ‍ca nahaituṃ naivasaikkhanāsaikkhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

349. haituṃ naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰…|

haituṃ  naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

350. nahaituyā dvai, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai  aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai nava, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

1-12. haituduka-parittattikaṃ

1. parittapadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

355. haituṃ parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nahaituṃ parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

haituṃ  parittañ‍ca nahaituṃ parittañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

356. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitunaārammaṇapac‍cayādi

357. nahaituṃ parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (2)

haituṃ parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati  naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ parittañ‍ca nahaituṃ parittañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

358. nahaituyā dvai, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai nava, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

haitupac‍cayā  naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayādi

401. haitu  appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau haitussa appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau haitussa appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

haitu appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau haitussa appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati … tīṇi|

nahaitu appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau nahaitussa appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

haitu appamāṇārammaṇau ca nahaitu appamāṇārammaṇau ca dhammā haitussa appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

402. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, atthiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

403. haitu  appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau haitussa appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

404. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

406. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai nava, āhārai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituṃ aniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ aniyatañ‍ca  nahaituṃ aniyatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

448. nahaituyā dvai, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai nava, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayādi

449. haitu aniyatau dhammau haitussa aniyatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu aniyatau dhammau haitussa aniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

haitu aniyatau dhammau haitussa aniyatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

nahaitu aniyatau dhammau nahaitussa aniyatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

haitu aniyatau ca nahaitu aniyatau ca dhammā haitussa aniyatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

450. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

451. haitu  aniyatau dhammau haitussa aniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

452. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

haitudukamicchattaniyatattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

1-16. haituduka-maggārammaṇattikaṃ

1. maggārammaṇapadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

453. haituṃ  maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

haituṃ maggārammaṇañ‍ca nahaituṃ maggārammaṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

454. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… kammai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayau

455. nahaituṃ  maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

456. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayādi

457. haitu maggārammaṇau dhammau haitussa maggārammaṇassa dhammassa  haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu  maggārammaṇau dhammau haitussa maggārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

nahaitu maggārammaṇau dhammau nahaitussa maggārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

haitu maggārammaṇau dhammau haitussa maggārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

458. haituyā tīṇi, adhipatiyā cha, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

haitu-upanissayapac‍cayā

477. haitu  uppan‍nau dhammau haitussa uppan‍nassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu uppan‍nau dhammau nahaitussa uppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu uppan‍nau dhammau haitussa uppan‍nassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

nahaitu uppan‍nau dhammau nahaitussa uppan‍nassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

haitu uppan‍nau dhammau haitussa uppan‍nassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitu uppan‍nau dhammau nahaitussa uppan‍nassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – uppan‍naṃ utuṃ upanissāya jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ… maggaṃ… abhiñ‍ñaṃ… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti (saṃkhittaṃ)|

478. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā cha, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā nava, avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

479. haitu  uppan‍nau dhammau haitussa uppan‍nassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

480. nahaituyā  nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ| imamhi dukatikai paṭic‍cavārampi sahajātavārampi pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi anuppan‍nampi uppādīpi natthi|)

haitudukaatītattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

1-19. haituduka-atītārammaṇattikaṃ

1. atītārammaṇapadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

485. haituṃ  atītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nahaituṃ atītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

486. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava…pai॰… kammai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitu-naadhipatipac‍cayā

487. nahaituṃ atītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ atītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (2)

haituṃ  atītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

488. nahaituyā dvai, naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi  pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayādi

489. haitu atītārammaṇau dhammau haitussa atītārammaṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu atītārammaṇau dhammau haitussa atītārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava|

haitu atītārammaṇau dhammau haitussa atītārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

nahaitu  atītārammaṇau dhammau nahaitussa atītārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi|

haitu  atītārammaṇau ca nahaitu atītārammaṇau ca dhammā haitussa atītārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

490. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava, atthiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

491. haitu atītārammaṇau dhammau haitussa atītārammaṇassa dhammassa  ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

492. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

nahaituṃ  anāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu anāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

494. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… kammai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

nahaitu-naadhipatipac‍cayā

527. nahaituṃ  ajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ ajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (2)

haituṃ ajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu ajjhattārammaṇau  dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

528. nahaituyā dvai, naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

pac‍canīyuddhārau

(yathā  kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

18. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai  cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha…pai॰… puraijātai dvai, āsaivanai dvai, kammai nava, vipākai nava…pai॰… vippayuttai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitu-naārammaṇapac‍cayā

19. ahaitukaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

sahaitukaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

ahaitukaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

sahaitukaṃ abyākatañ‍ca ahaitukaṃ abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

sahaitukaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

20. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai dvai, navipākai  pañ‍ca, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi  pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

22. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā cattāri, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai satta, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nissayai satta, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai dvai, pacchājātai dvai, āsaivanai dvai, kammai cattāri, vipākai cattāri, āhārai cattāri, indriyai cattāri, jhānai cattāri, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā satta…pai॰… avigatai satta (saṃkhittaṃ)|

64. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… kammai nava, vipākai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatipac‍cayau

65. haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu caiva sahaitukau ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

66. naadhipatiyā  nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, navippayuttai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā  naadhipatiyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatipac‍cayā haituyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavārau sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

68. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

nahaituṃ ahaitukaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ahaitukau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

appac‍cayau abyākatau dhammau sappac‍cayassa abyākatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā dvai, anantarai aikaṃ…pai॰… nissayai aikaṃ, upanissayai dvai, puraijātai aikaṃ, pacchājātai aikaṃ, (sabbattha aikaṃ), avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

appac‍cayau abyākatau dhammau sappac‍cayassa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

2. akusalapadaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

18. haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ| (sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ|)

20. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai aikaṃ, adhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā tīṇi (sabbattha tīṇi), nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, naāhārai tīṇi…pai॰… nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

sanidassanau abyākatau dhammau anidassanassa abyākatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati| (1)

anidassanau abyākatau dhammau anidassanassa abyākatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| anidassanau abyākatau dhammau sanidassanassa abyākatassa ca anidassanassa abyākatassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātādhipati| (2)

sanidassanau  abyākatau dhammau anidassanassa abyākatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pakatūpanissayau| (1)

anidassanau abyākatau dhammau anidassanassa abyākatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

1. kusalapadaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

9. sāsavaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

anāsavaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anāsavau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

10. haituyā  dvai, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā dvai…pai॰… avigatai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ, anulaumaṃ)|

11. naadhipatiyā dvai…pai॰… naāsaivanai aikaṃ…pai॰… navippayuttai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ| pac‍canīyaṃ| sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… sampayuttavāraipi sabbattha dvai)|

7. pañhāvārau

haitupac‍cayau

12. sāsavau  kusalau dhammau sāsavassa kusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anāsavau kusalau dhammau anāsavassa kusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

13. haituyā  dvai, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai dvai…pai॰… nissayai dvai, upanissayai cattāri, āsaivanai dvai…pai॰… avigatai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

2. akusalapadaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

14. sāsavaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

15. haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraipi …pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ|)

3. abyākatapadaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

16. sāsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

anāsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anāsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anāsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anāsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau abyākatau ca anāsavau abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

sāsavaṃ abyākatañ‍ca anāsavaṃ abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

17. haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca…pai॰… āsaivanai aikaṃ…pai॰… vipākai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā dvai, napuraijātai cattāri, napacchājātai naāsaivanai pañ‍ca, nakammai…pai॰… namaggai aikaṃ…pai॰… navippayuttai dvai…pai॰… nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi…pai॰… sampayuttavāraupi sabbattha vitthāraitabbau|)

7. pañhāvārau

haitupac‍cayau

18. sāsavau  abyākatau dhammau sāsavassa abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anāsavau abyākatau dhammau anāsavassa abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

19. haituyā cattāri, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā anantarai samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai satta, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai pacchājātai dvai, āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai…pai॰… maggai cattāri, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai satta (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

sāsavadukakusalattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

16-1. āsavasampayuttaduka-kusalattikaṃ

1. kusalapadaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

haitupac‍cayau

20. āsavavippayuttaṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

21. haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ)|

2. akusalapadaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

22. āsavasampayuttaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavasampayuttau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

āsavavippayuttaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavasampayuttau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

āsavasampayuttaṃ  akusalañ‍ca āsavavippayuttaṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavasampayuttau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

23. haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… sampayuttavāraipi sabbattha pañ‍ca|)

nahaituyā  aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā pañ‍ca, napuraijātai pañ‍ca…pai॰… nakammai tīṇi, navipākai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

24. āsavasampayuttau akusalau dhammau āsavasampayuttassa akusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

āsavavippayuttau  akusalau dhammau āsavasampayuttassa akusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

āsavasampayuttau akusalau ca āsavavippayuttau akusalau ca dhammā āsavasampayuttassa akusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

25. haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā aikaṃ, anantarai samanantarai nava, sahajātai añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nissayai pañ‍ca, upanissayai āsaivanai nava, kammai āhārai indriyai jhānai maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

3. abyākatapadaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

26. āsavavippayuttaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

27. haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā  aikaṃ, naārammaṇai aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā aikaṃ…pai॰… nauvigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… sampayuttavāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ)|

28. āsavavippayuttau  abyākatau dhammau āsavavippayuttassa abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

29. haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

āsavasampayuttadukakusalattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

17-1. āsavasāsavaduka-kusalattikaṃ

1. kusalapadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

haitupac‍cayau

30. sāsavañ‍caiva  nau ca āsavaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau caiva nau ca āsavau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

31. haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ|)

2. akusalapadaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

32. āsavañ‍caiva  sāsavañ‍ca akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau caiva sāsavau ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau caiva nau ca āsavau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

āsavañ‍caiva sāsavañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau caiva sāsavau ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

33. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayau

34. sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau caiva sāsavau ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

35. nahaituyā  aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, navippayuttai  nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

haitupac‍cayau

36. āsavau  caiva sāsavauca akusalau dhammau āsavassa caiva sāsavassa ca akusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

37. haituyā satta, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… upanissayai āsaivanai nava, kammai āhārai indriyai jhānai tīṇi, maggai sampayuttai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

38. āsavau caiva sāsavau ca akusalau dhammau āsavassa caiva sāsavassa ca akusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

39. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai satta (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

3. abyākatapadaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

40. sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau caiva nau ca āsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

41. haituyā  aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraipi …pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ|)

āsavasāsavadukakusalattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

18-1. āsavaāsavasampayuttaduka-kusalattikaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

akusalapadaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

42. āsavañ‍caiva  āsavasampayuttañ‍ca akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau caiva āsavasampayuttau ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

āsavasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavasampayuttau caiva nau ca āsavau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

āsavañ‍caiva āsavasampayuttañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca āsavasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau caiva āsavasampayuttau ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

43. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ

naadhipatipac‍cayau

44. āsavañ‍caiva  āsavasampayuttañ‍ca akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau caiva āsavasampayuttau ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā|

45. naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, navippayuttai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraipi… sampayuttavāraipi sabbattha nava|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

46. āsavau caiva āsavasampayuttau ca akusalau dhammau āsavassa caiva āsavasampayuttassa ca akusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

47. haituyā cattāri, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā  nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cattāri (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā  kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

āsavaāsavasampayuttadukakusalattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

19-1. āsavavippayuttasāsavaduka-kusalattikaṃ

kusalapadaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

48. āsavavippayuttaṃ sāsavaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau sāsavau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| āsavavippayuttaṃ anāsavaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau anāsavau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (2) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

49. haituyā  dvai, ārammaṇai dvai…pai॰… avigatai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi…pai॰… pañhāvāraupi vitthāraitabbau|)

abyākatapadaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

50. āsavavippayuttaṃ sāsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau sāsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

āsavavippayuttaṃ anāsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau anāsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| āsavavippayuttaṃ anāsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau sāsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| āsavavippayuttaṃ anāsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau sāsavau abyākatau ca āsavavippayuttau anāsavau abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

āsavavippayuttaṃ  sāsavaṃ abyākatañ‍ca āsavavippayuttaṃ anāsavaṃ abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau sāsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

51. haituyā  pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca…pai॰… āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai pañ‍ca, vipākai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayau

52. āsavavippayuttaṃ sāsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau sāsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

53. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā dvai  …pai॰… napuraijātai cattāri, napacchājātai naāsaivanai pañ‍ca, nakammai navipākai…pai॰… namaggai aikaṃ…pai॰… navippayuttai dvai…pai॰… nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

54. āsavavippayuttau sāsavau abyākatau dhammau āsavavippayuttassa sāsavassa abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

āsavavippayuttau  anāsavau abyākatau dhammau āsavavippayuttassa anāsavassa abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| āsavavippayuttau anāsavau abyākatau dhammau āsavavippayuttassa sāsavassa abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| āsavavippayuttau anāsavau abyākatau dhammau āsavavippayuttassa sāsavassa abyākatassa ca āsavavippayuttassa anāsavassa abyākatassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

āsavavippayuttau sāsavau abyākatau dhammau āsavavippayuttassa sāsavassa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

āsavavippayuttau  anāsavau abyākatau dhammau āsavavippayuttassa anāsavassa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| āsavavippayuttau anāsavau abyākatau dhammau āsavavippayuttassa sāsavassa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

55. haituyā cattāri, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā cattāri, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai satta, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai dvai, āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai cattāri, vipākai cattāri, āhārai cattāri…pai॰… avigatai satta (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

56. āsavavippayuttau sāsavau abyākatau dhammau āsavavippayuttassa sāsavassa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

57. nahaituyā satta, naārammaṇai satta (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai cattāri (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

āsavavippayuttasāsavadukakusalattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

āsavagaucchakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

20-1. sañ‍ñaujanaduka-kusalattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

1. nausañ‍ñaujanaṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nausañ‍ñaujanau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

2. haituyā  aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ|)

3. sañ‍ñaujanaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sañ‍ñaujanau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sañ‍ñaujanaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nausañ‍ñaujanau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sañ‍ñaujanaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sañ‍ñaujanau akusalau ca nausañ‍ñaujanau akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

4. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava…pai॰… nakammai tīṇi…pai॰… navippayuttai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

sañ‍ñaujanagaucchakakusalattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

26-1. ganthaduka-kusalattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

1. nauganthaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauganthau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

2. haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraipi …pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ)|

3. ganthaṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ganthau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| ganthaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauganthau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| ganthaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ganthau akusalau ca nauganthau akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nauganthaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauganthau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

ganthaṃ akusalañ‍ca nauganthaṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ganthau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

4. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā  aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, navippayuttai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraupi…pai॰… sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

5. ganthau akusalau dhammau ganthassa akusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

6. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… kammai āhārai indriyai jhānai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

7. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā  kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

8. nauganthaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauganthau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

31. parāmāsavippayuttaṃ  parāmaṭṭhaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parāmāsavippayuttau parāmaṭṭhau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

55-1. sārammaṇaduka-kusalattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

1. sārammaṇaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sārammaṇau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (yathā sappac‍cayaṃ kusalaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

2. sārammaṇaṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sārammaṇau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (yathā sappac‍cayaṃ akusalaṃ, aivaṃ kātabbaṃ|)

3. sārammaṇaṃ  abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sārammaṇau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

anārammaṇaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anārammaṇau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

sārammaṇaṃ abyākatañ‍ca anārammaṇaṃ abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sārammaṇau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

4. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca…pai॰… añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha…pai॰… puraijātai aikaṃ, āsaivanai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava…pai॰… nakammai dvai, navipākai pañ‍ca, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai dvai, namaggai  nava, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitu-ārammaṇa-adhipatipac‍cayā

5. sārammaṇau abyākatau dhammau sārammaṇassa abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

6. sārammaṇau abyākatau dhammau sārammaṇassa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anārammaṇau  abyākatau dhammau sārammaṇassa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau|

7. sārammaṇau abyākatau dhammau sārammaṇassa abyākatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… tīṇi| anārammaṇau abyākatau dhammau sārammaṇassa abyākatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati (saṃkhittaṃ)|

8. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā cattāri, anantarai aikaṃ…pai॰… sahajātai satta, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nissayai satta, upanissayai dvai, puraijātai aikaṃ, pacchājātai aikaṃ, āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai tīṇi, vipākai tīṇi, sampayuttai aikaṃ, vippayuttai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

56-1. cittaduka-kusalattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

9. cittaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

naucittaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naucittaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca cittau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naucittaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca cittau kusalau ca naucittau kusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

cittaṃ  kusalañ‍ca naucittaṃ kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca naucittau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

10. haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai pañ‍ca (sabbattha pañ‍ca), avigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatiyā  pañ‍ca, napuraijātai pañ‍ca, napacchājātai pañ‍ca, naāsaivanai pañ‍ca, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

11. naucittau kusalau dhammau naucittassa kusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naucittau kusalau dhammau cittassa kusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naucittau kusalau dhammau cittassa kusalassa ca naucittassa kusalassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

12. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava…pai॰… sahajātai añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nissayai pañ‍ca, upanissayai āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, āhārai indriyai pañ‍ca, jhānai maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

13. cittaṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

naucittaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naucittaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca cittau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naucittaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca cittau akusalau ca naucittau akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

cittaṃ  akusalañ‍ca naucittaṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

14. haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca| (saṃkhittaṃ| cittadukakusalasadisaṃ, nahaituyāpi kattabbaṃ| sahajātavārampi…pai॰… pañhāvārampi kātabbaṃ)|

15. cittaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

naucittaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naucittaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca cittau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naucittaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca cittau abyākatau ca naucittau abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

cittaṃ  abyākatañ‍ca naucittaṃ abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

16. haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai pañ‍ca…pai॰… vipākai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā pañ‍ca, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā pañ‍ca…pai॰… napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai pañ‍ca, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai pañ‍ca, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai pañ‍ca, namaggai pañ‍ca, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai pañ‍ca…pai॰… nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

17. naucittau abyākatau dhammau naucittassa abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (cittadukakusalasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

55. cittasahabhuṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasahabhū akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naucittasahabhuṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasahabhū akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… pañhāvārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

naucittasahabhuṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca naucittasahabhū abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

70. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… puraijātai pañ‍ca, āsaivanai pañ‍ca, kammai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā  nava, naārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… nakammai cattāri, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai cha, namaggai nava, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai cha, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

72. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava (aittha chasu sahajātādhipati), anantarai nava (sabbattha nava), puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai nava, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai pañ‍ca, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

91. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava (ajjhattikau bāhirassa gamanakālai sahajātādhipati, majjhai tīsupi sahajātādhipati), anantarai nava, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, nissayai nava, upanissayai nava, puraijātai nava (ārammaṇapuraijātampi vatthupuraijātampi), pacchājātai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai nava (tiṇṇan‍naṃ kabaḷīkārau āhārau), indriyai nava (tiṇṇan‍naṃ rūpajīvitindriyaṃ), jhānai maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai pañ‍ca, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

upādā abyākatañ‍ca nauupādā abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauupādā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

95. haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… añ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca…pai॰… puraijātai aikaṃ, āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai pañ‍ca, vipākai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā pañ‍ca, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā pañ‍ca…pai॰… napuraijātai pañ‍ca…pai॰… nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, naāhārai tīṇi, naindriyai tīṇi, najhānai tīṇi, namaggai pañ‍ca, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haituārammaṇapac‍cayādi

96. nauupādā  abyākatau dhammau nauupādā abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

upādā abyākatau dhammau nauupādā abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nauupādā abyākatau dhammau nauupādā abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nauupādā abyākatau dhammau nauupādā abyākatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (paṭhamai dvaipi adhipatī, dvīsu sahajātādhipati)|

upādā  abyākatau dhammau nauupādā abyākatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nauupādā abyākatau dhammau nauupādā abyākatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

97. upanissayai  dvai, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai tīṇi, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai cha, indriyai satta, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai aikaṃ, vippayuttai cattāri, atthiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

68-1. upādin‍naduka-kusalattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

98. anupādin‍naṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (sabbattha aikaṃ| saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi aikaṃ sappac‍cayakusalasadisaṃ)|

99. anupādin‍naṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haituyā aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ)|

100. upādin‍naṃ  abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

anupādin‍naṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

upādin‍naṃ abyākatañ‍ca anupādin‍naṃ abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – upādin‍nai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

101. haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā aikaṃ…pai॰… puraijātai dvai, āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai pañ‍ca, vipākai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā  pañ‍ca, naārammaṇai cattāri, naadhipatiyā pañ‍ca…pai॰… napuraijātai cattāri, napacchājātai naāsaivanai pañ‍ca, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai dvai, naāhārai dvai, naindriyai dvai, najhānai dvai, namaggai pañ‍ca, nasampayuttai cattāri, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā cattāri, nauvigatai cattāri (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārādi vitthāraitabbau)|

haitu-puraijātapac‍cayā

102. upādin‍nau abyākatau dhammau upādin‍nassa abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

anupādin‍nau abyākatau dhammau anupādin‍nassa abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anupādin‍nau abyākatau dhammau anupādin‍nassa abyākatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati… aikaṃ|

upādin‍nau  abyākatau dhammau upādin‍nassa abyākatassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ (dvai pañhā)|

anupādin‍nau abyākatau dhammau anupādin‍nassa abyākatassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai (ārammaṇapuraijātaṃyaiva, ghaṭanā dvai, ārammaṇapuraijātampi vatthupuraijātampi)|

103. haituyā cattāri, ārammaṇai cattāri (upādin‍namūlakai dvai, anupādin‍namūlakai dvai), adhipatiyā aikaṃ, anantarai  samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai pañ‍ca, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai cha, pacchājātai cha, āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai cattāri, vipākai cattāri, āhārai nava, indriyai cattāri, jhānai cattāri, maggai cattāri, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai cha, atthiyā nava, natthiyā cattāri, vigatai cattāri, avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naukilaisaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naukilaisau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

kilaisaṃ akusalañ‍ca naukilaisaṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

3. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava (sabbattha nava), avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

4. kilaisaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchaṃ paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau, uddhac‍caṃ paṭic‍ca uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau (1)|

naukilaisaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai  uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

kilaisaṃ akusalañ‍ca naukilaisaṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kilaisau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca vicikicchañ‍ca paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau, uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca uddhac‍cañ‍ca paṭic‍ca uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

5. nahaituyā tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, navippayuttai nava (saṃkhittaṃ, sahajātavārādi vitthāraitabbau)|

6. kilaisau  akusalau dhammau kilaisassa akusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

7. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava (majjhai tīṇi sahajātādhipati), anantarai nava, samanantarai nava…pai॰… upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai nava, sampayuttai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naukilaisaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naukilaisau abyākatau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

8. haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ|)

85-1. dassanainapahātabbahaitukaduka-kusalattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

11. nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ kusalaṃ dhamma paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ|)

12. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ akusalañ‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi (nadassanai dvai, ghaṭanai aikaṃ, saṃkhittaṃ)|

13. haituyā cattāri, ārammaṇai cha, adhipatiyā dvai (sabbattha cha), avigatai cha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

14. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā|

nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

15. nahaituyā dvai, naadhipatiyā cha…pai॰… nakammai cattāri, navipākai cha, navippayuttai cha (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārādi vitthāraitabbau)|

16. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau akusalau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa akusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

17. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā tīṇi (dassanai aikaṃ, nadassanai dvai), anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai cha…pai॰… upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai cattāri, āhārai cattāri, indriyai cattāri…pai॰… sampayuttai cha, atthiyā cha, natthiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai cha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

18. nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ|)

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

19. nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haituyā  aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ)|

20. bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

nabhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

21. haituyā cattāri, ārammaṇai cha, adhipatiyā dvai…pai॰… avigatai cha (saṃkhittaṃ| dassanaina pahātabbahaitukadukaakusalasadisaṃ| sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

22. nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ|)

87-1. savitak‍kaduka-kusalattikaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

23. savitak‍kaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| savitak‍kaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| savitak‍kaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau kusalau ca avitak‍kau kusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

avitak‍kaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| avitak‍kaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (2)

savitak‍kaṃ kusalañ‍ca avitak‍kaṃ kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

24. haituyā cha, ārammaṇai cha (sabbattha cha), avigatai cha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatiyā cha, napuraijātai cha…pai॰… nakammai cattāri…pai॰… navippayuttai cha (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

7. pañhāvārau

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

25. savitak‍kau  kusalau dhammau savitak‍kassa kusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| savitak‍kau kusalau dhammau avitak‍kassa kusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kau kusalau dhammau savitak‍kassa kusalassa ca avitak‍kassa kusalassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kau kusalau dhammau avitak‍kassa kusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kau kusalau dhammau savitak‍kassa kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

avitak‍kau  kusalau dhammau avitak‍kassa kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

savitak‍kau kusalau ca avitak‍kau kusalau ca dhammā savitak‍kassa kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

26. haituyā cattāri, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava (haiṭṭhā tīsu sahajātādhipati, avitak‍kai aikaṃ, sahajātādhipati), anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai cha…pai॰… upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai cattāri, āhārai cattāri, indriyai cattāri, jhānai cha, maggai cha, sampayuttai cha, atthiyā cha, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai cha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cattāri (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

akusalapadaṃ

27. savitak‍kaṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| savitak‍kaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kau akusalau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| savitak‍kaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau akusalau ca avitak‍kau akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

avitak‍kaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

savitak‍kaṃ  akusalañ‍ca avitak‍kaṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

28. haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai pañ‍ca (sabbattha pañ‍ca)…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

29. savitak‍kaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

avitak‍kaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

savitak‍kaṃ akusalañ‍ca avitak‍kaṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

30. nahaituyā tīṇi, naadhipatiyā pañ‍ca, nakammai tīṇi…pai॰… navippayuttai pañ‍ca| (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārādi vitthāraitabbau|)

31. savitak‍kau akusalau dhammau savitak‍kassa akusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

32. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai nava…pai॰… sahajātai pañ‍ca…pai॰… upanissayai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi…pai॰… jhānai pañ‍ca, maggai pañ‍ca, sampayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā pañ‍ca, natthiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

abyākatapadaṃ

33. savitak‍kaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

avitak‍kaṃ  abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

savitak‍kaṃ abyākatañ‍ca avitak‍kaṃ abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

34. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… puraijātai āsaivanai cha…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava…pai॰… napuraijātai nava…pai॰… nakammai cattāri, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai nava, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai cha…pai॰… nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

35. savitak‍kau abyākatau dhammau savitak‍kassa abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

savitak‍kau abyākatau dhammau savitak‍kassa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

65. haituyā  dvai, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā tīṇi (kāmāvacarai aikaṃ, nakāmāvacarai dvai), anantarai tīṇi (kāmāvacarai dvai, nakāmāvacarai aikaṃ)…pai॰… sahajātai dvai …pai॰… upanissayai cattāri, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai dvai, āhārai dvai…pai॰… natthiyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā  aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā nava…pai॰… nakammai dvai, navipākai pañ‍ca, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārādi vitthāraitabbau)|

araṇaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

1. kusalaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

akusalaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

2. kusalaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

akusalaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

3. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ –naadhipatipac‍cayau

12. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā satta, anantarai pañ‍ca, samanantarai pañ‍ca, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, indriyai dvai, maggai dvai, sampayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā pañ‍ca, vigatai pañ‍ca, avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

13. kusalau haitu dhammau kusalassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau haitu dhammau akusalassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau  haitu  dhammau abyākatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

akusalau haitu dhammau akusalassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau haitu dhammau kusalassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau haitu dhammau abyākatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

abyākatau haitu dhammau abyākatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

14. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

2. nahaitupadaṃ

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

15. kusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau nahaitu ca abyākatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

akusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau nahaitu ca abyākatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

abyākataṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

16. kusalaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)
