17. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai nava, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai nava, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai nava, indriyai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitunaārammaṇapac‍cayādi

18. abyākataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

kusalaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

akusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

kusalaṃ  nahaituñ‍ca abyākataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

akusalaṃ  nahaituñ‍ca abyākataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

19. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisā vitthāraitabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

20. kusalau nahaitu dhammau kusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau nahaitu dhammau akusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa  ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau nahaitu dhammau abyākatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

akusalau nahaitu dhammau akusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau nahaitu dhammau kusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau nahaitu dhammau abyākatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

abyākatau nahaitu dhammau abyākatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatau nahaitu dhammau kusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatau nahaitu dhammau akusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

21. kusalau  nahaitu dhammau kusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau nahaitu dhammau akusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau nahaitu dhammau abyākatassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau nahaitu dhammau kusalassa nahaitussa ca abyākatassa nahaitussa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

akusalau nahaitu dhammau akusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

abyākatau nahaitu dhammau abyākatassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatau nahaitu dhammau kusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

22. kusalau nahaitu dhammau kusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau nahaitu dhammau abyākatassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

akusalau nahaitu dhammau akusalassa  nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau nahaitu dhammau abyākatassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

abyākatau nahaitu dhammau abyākatassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi…pai॰…|

upanissayapac‍cayau

23. kusalau nahaitu dhammau kusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau nahaitu dhammau akusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau nahaitu dhammau abyākatassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

akusalau nahaitu dhammau akusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau nahaitu dhammau kusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau nahaitu dhammau abyākatassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

abyākatau  nahaitu dhammau abyākatassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatau nahaitu dhammau kusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatau nahaitu dhammau akusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

24. ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā dasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai satta, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tairasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā pan‍narasa, naārammaṇai pan‍narasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayā nahaituyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

2-1. vaidanāttika-haitudukaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

25. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

adukkhamasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

26. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… vipākai dvai…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatiyā  tīṇi, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā  naadhipatiyā tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatipac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

27. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

28. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

dukkhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

29. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā cattāri, anantarai cha, samanantarai cha, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai tīṇi, vipākai dvai, indriyai dvai, maggai dvai, sampayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā cha, vigatai cha, avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā  nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

32. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa  dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

adukkhamasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

33. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

anantara-upanissayapac‍cayā

34. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi…pai॰…|

35. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

adukkhamasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

36. ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai aṭṭha, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayā nahaituyā nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

3-1. vipākattika-haitudukaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

37. vipākaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

vipākadhammadhammaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

38. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… āsaivanai dvai, kammai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatiyā  tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, navipākai dvai, navippayuttai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

39. vipākau  haitu dhammau vipākassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau vipākadhammadhammassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

40. vipākau haitu dhammau vipākassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau haitu dhammau vipākadhammadhammassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau haitu dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

vipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau vipākadhammadhammassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau vipākassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

41. vipākau haitu dhammau vipākassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau  haitu dhammau vipākadhammadhammassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau haitu dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau … tīṇi|

vipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau vipākadhammadhammassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

42. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā cha, anantarai pañ‍ca, samanantarai pañ‍ca, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai dvai, vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nahaitupadapac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

43. vipākaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau nahaitu ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

vipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| vipākadhammadhammaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau nahaitu ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

vipākaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

vipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

44. haituyā tairasa, ārammaṇai pañ‍ca, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai pañ‍ca, samanantarai pañ‍ca, sahajātai tairasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai pañ‍ca, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai dvai, kammai tairasa, vipākai nava, āhārai tairasa…pai॰… sampayuttai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai tairasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitu-naārammaṇapac‍cayā

45. vipākaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā… nava|

vipākaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

46. nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā tairasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai  pañ‍ca, napuraijātai dvādasa, napacchājātai tairasa, naāsaivanai tairasa, nakammai dvai, navipākai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi…pai॰… nauvigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

47. vipākau nahaitu dhammau vipākassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau nahaitu dhammau vipākadhammadhammassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau nahaitu dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

vipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau vipākadhammadhammassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

48. vipākau nahaitu dhammau vipākassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

vipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau vipākadhammadhammassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau  nahaitu dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau vipākassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau vipākadhammadhammassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

49. vipākau  nahaitu dhammau vipākassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

vipākadhammadhammau  nahaitu dhammau vipākadhammadhammassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

50. ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā dasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai aikādasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai dvai, kammai nava, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai satta, indriyai nava, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa…pai॰… avigatai tairasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā sauḷasa, naārammaṇai sauḷasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

4-1. upādin‍nattika-haitudukaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

51. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

52. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā dvai…pai॰… vipākai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi…pai॰… naāsaivanai tīṇi, navipākai dvai, navippayuttai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

53. upādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau upādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau anupādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyau haitu dhammau anupādin‍naanupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

54. upādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau upādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau anupādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

anupādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau anupādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anupādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau upādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyau  haitu  dhammau anupādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

55. upādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau anupādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau anupādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyau haitu dhammau anupādin‍naanupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anupādin‍naanupādāniyau haitu  dhammau anupādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anantara-upanissayapac‍cayā

56. upādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau upādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau anupādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

anupādin‍naanupādāniyau haitu dhammau anupādin‍naanupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anupādin‍naanupādāniyau haitu dhammau upādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (2)

57. upādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau upādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau anupādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa  upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau anupādin‍naanupādāniyassa  haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

anupādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau anupādin‍nupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

anupādin‍naanupādāniyau haitu dhammau anupādin‍naanupādāniyassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

58. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai pañ‍ca, adhipatiyā cattāri, anantarai cha, samanantarai cha, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai dvai, vipākai dvai…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nahaitupadapac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

59. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ nahaituñ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ nahaituñ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

60. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

61. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai nava, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai dvai, kammai nava, vipākai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ – nahaitupac‍cayau

62. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ nahaituñ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

63. nahaituyā pañ‍ca, naārammaṇai cha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

5-1. saṃkiliṭṭhattika-haitudukaṃ

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

64. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

65. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatiyā  tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|) (sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haituārammaṇapac‍cayādi

66. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau haitu  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

67. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

68. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  haitu dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

69. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  haitu dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cha…pai॰…|

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau haitu  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

70. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai pañ‍ca, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai cha, samanantarai cha, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai aṭṭha, āsaivanai tīṇi, vipākai dvai, indriyai dvai…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā aṭṭha, naārammaṇai aṭṭha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā  kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nahaitupadapac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

71. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu ca dhammā  uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ nahaituñ‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ nahaituñ‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

72. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

73. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai nava, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai nava, vipākai pañ‍ca, āhārai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ –nahaitupac‍cayau

74. asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā  aikaṃ, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā cha, napuraijātai satta…pai॰… nakammai tīṇi…pai॰… navippayuttai tīṇi…pai॰… nauvigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

ārammaṇa-adhipatipac‍cayā

75. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  nahaitu dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau  nahaitu dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

76. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

77. ārammaṇai cha, adhipatiyā aṭṭha, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai aṭṭha, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai satta, vipākai cattāri, āhārai satta…pai॰… avigatai tairasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā cuddasa, naārammaṇai cuddasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayā  nahaituyā cha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai cha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

6-1. vitak‍kattika-haitudukaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

78. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

79. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā sahajātavārampi… sampayuttavārampi aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

80. savitak‍kasavicārau haitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau haitu dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau haitu dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

81. savitak‍kasavicārau  haitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārau haitu dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  haitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattau haitu dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau haitu dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicārau haitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

82. savitak‍kasavicārau haitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  haitu dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattau haitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau haitu dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicārau haitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

83. savitak‍kasavicārau haitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

avitak‍kavicāramattau haitu dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau … tīṇi|

avitak‍kaavicārau haitu dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi…pai॰…|

savitak‍kasavicārau  haitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

84. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai cha, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai pañ‍ca, vipākai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai cha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nahaitupadaṃ|

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

85. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (aikaṃ)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (dvai)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (tīṇi)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (cattāri)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca  avitak‍kaavicārau  nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (pañ‍ca)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (cha)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (satta)|

86. avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (aikaṃ)| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (dvai)| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (tīṇi)| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (cattāri)| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (pañ‍ca)|

87. avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (aikaṃ)| avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (dvai)| avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (tīṇi)| avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā (cattāri)| avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (pañ‍ca)| avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (cha)| avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kavicāramattau  nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (satta)|

88. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (aikaṃ)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ  nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (dvai)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (tīṇi)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (cattāri)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (pañ‍ca)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā (cha)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (satta)|

89. avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (aikaṃ)| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (dvai)| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (tīṇi)| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (cattāri)| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (pañ‍ca)|

90. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (aikaṃ)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (dvai)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā (tīṇi)|

91. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (aikaṃ)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (dvai)| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (tīṇi)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

92. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… cattāri|

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… cattāri|

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… aikaṃ|

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nahaituñ‍ca avitak‍kaavicāraṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

93. haituyā sattatiṃsa, ārammaṇai aikavīsa, adhipatiyā taivīsa, anantarai aikavīsa, sahajātai sattatiṃsa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aṭṭhavīsa, nissayai sattatiṃsa, upanissayai aikavīsa, puraijātai aikādasa, āsaivanai aikādasa, kammai sattatiṃsa, vipākai āhārai indriyai jhānai maggai sattatiṃsa, sampayuttai aikavīsa, vippayuttai sattatiṃsa…pai॰… avigatai sattatiṃsa, (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā taittiṃsa, naārammaṇai satta (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai satta (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai cuddasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

94. savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa  nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa nahaitussa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa nahaitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

95. avitak‍kavicāramattau  nahaitu dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa nahaitussa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa nahaitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

96. avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa nahaitussa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa nahaitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa nahaitussa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa nahaitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca|

97. avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa nahaitussa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa nahaitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

98. savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa  nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārau  nahaitu ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa nahaitussa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa nahaitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

adhipatipac‍cayau

99. savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca|

avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca|

avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca avitak‍kaavicārau nahaitu ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu ca avitak‍kavicāramattau nahaitu ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

anantarapac‍cayādi

100. savitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

savitak‍kasavicārau  nahaitu dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

101. ārammaṇai aikavīsa, adhipatiyā taivīsa, anantarai pañ‍cavīsa, samanantarai pañ‍cavīsa, sahajātai tiṃsa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aṭṭhavīsa, nissayai tiṃsa, upanissayai pañ‍cavīsa, puraijātai pañ‍ca, pacchājātai pañ‍ca, āsaivanai aikavīsa, kammai aikādasa, vipākai aikavīsa, āhārai aikādasa…pai॰… avigatai tiṃsa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā  pañ‍catiṃsa, naārammaṇai pañ‍catiṃsa, naadhipatiyā  pañ‍catiṃsa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayā nahaituyā aikavīsa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikavīsa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

7-1. pītittika-haitudukaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

102. pītisahagataṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

sukhasahagataṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

upaikkhāsahagataṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upaikkhāsahagatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… aikaṃ|

pītisahagataṃ  haituñ‍ca sukhasahagataṃ haituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

103. haituyā dasa, ārammaṇai dasa…pai॰… avigatai dasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatiyā dasa, napuraijātai dasa, napacchājātai dasa, naāsaivanai dasa, navipākai dasa, navippayuttai dasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haituārammaṇapac‍cayādi

104. pītisahagatau haitu dhammau pītisahagatassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

sukhasahagatau  haitu dhammau sukhasahagatassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

upaikkhāsahagatau  haitu dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… aikaṃ|

pītisahagatau haitu ca sukhasahagatau haitu ca dhammā pītisahagatassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

105. pītisahagatau haitu dhammau pītisahagatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagatau haitu dhammau sukhasahagatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagatau haitu dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagatau haitu dhammau pītisahagatassa haitussa ca sukhasahagatassa haitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

sukhasahagatau haitu dhammau sukhasahagatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhasahagatau haitu dhammau pītisahagatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhasahagatau haitu dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhasahagatau haitu dhammau pītisahagatassa haitussa ca sukhasahagatassa haitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

upaikkhāsahagatau haitu dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upaikkhāsahagatau haitu dhammau pītisahagatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upaikkhāsahagatau haitu dhammau sukhasahagatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upaikkhāsahagatau haitu dhammau pītisahagatassa haitussa ca sukhasahagatassa haitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

pītisahagatau haitu ca sukhasahagatau haitu ca dhammā pītisahagatassa haitussa  dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

pītisahagatau  haitu dhammau pītisahagatassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

106. haituyā dasa, ārammaṇai sauḷasa, adhipatiyā sauḷasa, anantarai sauḷasa, samanantarai sauḷasa, sahajātai dasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dasa, nissayai dasa, upanissayai sauḷasa…pai॰… vipākai dasa…pai॰… avigatai dasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā sauḷasa, naārammaṇai sauḷasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai dasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai sauḷasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nahaitupadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

107. pītisahagataṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| pītisahagataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| pītisahagataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau nahaitu ca sukhasahagatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

sukhasahagataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sukhasahagataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sukhasahagataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau nahaitu ca sukhasahagatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

upaikkhāsahagataṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upaikkhāsahagatau nahaitudhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

pītisahagataṃ nahaituñ‍ca sukhasahagataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| pītisahagataṃ nahaituñ‍ca sukhasahagataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatau nahaitu dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| pītisahagataṃ nahaituñ‍ca sukhasahagataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau nahaitu ca sukhasahagatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

108. pītisahagataṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

sukhasahagataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

upaikkhāsahagataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upaikkhāsahagatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… aikaṃ|

pītisahagataṃ nahaituñ‍ca sukhasahagataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

109. haituyā dasa, ārammaṇai dasa, adhipatiyā dasa, anantarai dasa…pai॰… avigatai dasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā dasa, naadhipatiyā dasa (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nahaitupadaṃ

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

110. pītisahagatau nahaitu dhammau pītisahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagatau nahaitu dhammau sukhasahagatassa  nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagatau nahaitu dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagatau nahaitu dhammau pītisahagatassa nahaitussa ca sukhasahagatassa nahaitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

sukhasahagatau nahaitu dhammau sukhasahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhasahagatau  nahaitu dhammau pītisahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhasahagatau  nahaitu dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhasahagatau nahaitu dhammau pītisahagatassa nahaitussa ca sukhasahagatassa nahaitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

upaikkhāsahagatau nahaitu dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upaikkhāsahagatau nahaitu dhammau pītisahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upaikkhāsahagatau nahaitu dhammau sukhasahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upaikkhāsahagatau nahaitu dhammau pītisahagatassa nahaitussa ca sukhasahagatassa nahaitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

pītisahagatau nahaitu ca sukhasahagatau nahaitu ca dhammā pītisahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

111. pītisahagatau nahaitu dhammau pītisahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa  adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

sukhasahagatau nahaitu dhammau sukhasahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

upaikkhāsahagatau nahaitu dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

pītisahagatau nahaitu ca sukhasahagatau nahaitu ca dhammā pītisahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

112. pītisahagatau nahaitu dhammau pītisahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

sukhasahagatau  nahaitu dhammau sukhasahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

upaikkhāsahagatau nahaitu dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri|

pītisahagatau  nahaitu ca sukhasahagatau nahaitu ca dhammā pītisahagatassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cattāri (saṃkhittaṃ)|

113. ārammaṇai sauḷasa, adhipatiyā sauḷasa, sahajātai dasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dasa, nissayai dasa, upanissayai sauḷasa, āhārai dasa…pai॰… avigatai dasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā sauḷasa, naārammaṇai sauḷasa, naadhipatiyā sauḷasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayā  nahaituyā sauḷasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai sauḷasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

8-1. dassanainapahātabbattika-haitudukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

114. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

115. haituyā tīṇi (sabbattha tīṇi), vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi…pai॰… navippayuttai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

116. dassanaina pahātabbau haitu dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

dassanaina  pahātabbau haitu dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

117. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai aṭṭha, adhipatiyā cha, anantarai pañ‍ca, samanantarai pañ‍ca, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai aṭṭha, āsaivanai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, maggai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā aṭṭha, naārammaṇai aṭṭha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aṭṭha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nahaitupadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

118. dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dassanaina pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dassanaina pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau nahaitu ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

119. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitu-naārammaṇapac‍cayā

120. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā|

dassanaina pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

121. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā nava…pai॰… nauvigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

122. dassanaina pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dassanaina pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

bhāvanāya pahātabbau  nahaitu dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

123. ārammaṇai aṭṭha, adhipatiyā dasa…pai॰… avigatai tairasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā  cuddasa, naārammaṇai cuddasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayā nahaituyā aṭṭha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aṭṭha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aṭṭha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

10-1. ācayagāmittika-haitudukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

132. ācayagāmiṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ācayagāmī haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

apacayagāmiṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca apacayagāmī haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

133. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi (sabbattha tīṇi)|

naadhipatiyā  tīṇi, naāsaivanai dvai, navipākai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatipac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haituārammaṇapac‍cayādi

134. ācayagāmī haitu dhammau ācayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

apacayagāmī  haitu dhammau apacayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī haitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ācayagāmī haitu dhammau ācayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| ācayagāmī haitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

apacayagāmī haitu dhammau ācayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| apacayagāmī haitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī haitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī haitu dhammau ācayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

135. ācayagāmī haitu dhammau ācayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| (1)

apacayagāmī haitu dhammau apacayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātādhipati…| apacayagāmī haitu dhammau ācayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| apacayagāmī haitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati…| (3)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī  haitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī haitu dhammau ācayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

136. ācayagāmī  haitu dhammau ācayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ācayagāmī haitu dhammau apacayagāmissa haitussa  dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ācayagāmī haitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

apacayagāmī haitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī haitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

137. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai cha, adhipatiyā cha, anantarai pañ‍ca, samanantarai pañ‍ca, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai aṭṭha, āsaivanai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

138. ācayagāmiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ācayagāmī nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| ācayagāmiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| ācayagāmiṃ nahaituṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ācayagāmī nahaitu ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

apacayagāmiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca apacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| apacayagāmiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| apacayagāmiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca apacayagāmī nahaitu ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

139. ācayagāmiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ācayagāmī nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

140. naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

142. ācayagāmī nahaitu dhammau ācayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ācayagāmī nahaitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

apacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau ācayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| apacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau ācayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau apacayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

143. ācayagāmī nahaitu dhammau ācayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

apacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau  apacayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| apacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau ācayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| apacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| apacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau apacayagāmissa nahaitussa ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa nahaitussa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

144. naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

ācayagāmī  nahaitu dhammau ācayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ācayagāmī nahaitu dhammau apacayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ācayagāmī nahaitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

apacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī nahaitu dhammau ācayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)…pai॰…|

ācayagāmī  nahaitu dhammau ācayagāmissa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

145. ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā dasa, anantarai cha samanantarai cha, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai nava…pai॰… avigatai tairasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā  pan‍narasa, naārammaṇai pan‍narasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayā nahaituyā satta (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai satta (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

11-1. saikkhattika-haitudukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

146. saikkhaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saikkhau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

asaikkhaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaikkhau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

147. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatiyā  tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, navipākai dvai, navippayuttai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatipac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau haitu dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

150. saikkhau  haitu dhammau saikkhassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saikkhau haitu dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaikkhau  haitu dhammau asaikkhassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| asaikkhau haitu dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau haitu dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

151. saikkhau haitu dhammau saikkhassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saikkhau haitu dhammau asaikkhassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saikkhau haitu dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

asaikkhau  haitu dhammau asaikkhassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| asaikkhau haitu dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau haitu dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau haitu dhammau saikkhassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau haitu dhammau asaikkhassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

152. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai aṭṭha, samanantarai aṭṭha, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai aṭṭha…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

153. saikkhaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saikkhau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saikkhaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saikkhaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saikkhau nahaitu ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

haitupac‍cayau

167. parittārammaṇaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittārammaṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatārammaṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇārammaṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi …pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

168. parittārammaṇau haitu dhammau parittārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa  haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇau haitu dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇau haitu dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittārammaṇau  haitu dhammau parittārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| parittārammaṇau haitu dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatārammaṇau haitu dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahaggatārammaṇau haitu dhammau parittārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇārammaṇau haitu dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| appamāṇārammaṇau haitu dhammau parittārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| appamāṇārammaṇau haitu dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

169. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā satta, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, upanissayai nava…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai satta (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

170. parittārammaṇaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

171. parittārammaṇaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

ārammaṇa-adhipatipac‍cayā

172. parittārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau parittārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| parittārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahaggatārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau parittārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| appamāṇārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau parittārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| appamāṇārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

parittārammaṇau  nahaitu dhammau parittārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

173. ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā satta, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, upanissayai nava…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

174. hīnaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca hīnau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| majjhimaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca majjhimau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| paṇītaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca paṇītau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

175. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

176. hīnau haitu dhammau hīnassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

majjhimau haitu dhammau majjhimassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

paṇītau haitu dhammau paṇītassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

hīnau haitu dhammau hīnassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| hīnau haitu dhammau majjhimassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

majjhimau  haitu dhammau majjhimassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| majjhimau haitu dhammau hīnassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

paṇītau haitu dhammau majjhimassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

177. hīnau  haitu dhammau hīnassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

majjhimau haitu dhammau majjhimassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| majjhimau haitu dhammau hīnassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

paṇītau haitu dhammau paṇītassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṇītau haitu dhammau majjhimassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

hīnau haitu dhammau hīnassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

majjhimau haitu dhammau majjhimassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai|

paṇītau haitu dhammau paṇītassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

178. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai pañ‍ca, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca…pai॰ … avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

180. micchattaniyataṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca micchattaniyatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

sammattaniyataṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sammattaniyatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

aniyataṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

181. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatiyā dvai, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai aikaṃ, navipākai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi  …pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

182. micchattaniyatau  haitu dhammau micchattaniyatassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sammattaniyatau haitu dhammau sammattaniyatassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau haitu dhammau aniyatassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

micchattaniyatau haitu dhammau aniyatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sammattaniyatau haitu dhammau aniyatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau haitu dhammau aniyatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau  haitu dhammau micchattaniyatassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sammattaniyatau haitu dhammau sammattaniyatassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sammattaniyatau haitu dhammau aniyatassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

aniyatau haitu dhammau aniyatassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anantarapac‍cayādi

aniyatau haitu dhammau aniyatassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatau haitu dhammau micchattaniyatassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatau haitu dhammau sammattaniyatassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

184. micchattaniyatau  haitu dhammau micchattaniyatassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| micchattaniyatau haitu dhammau aniyatassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

sammattaniyatau haitu dhammau sammattaniyatassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sammattaniyatau haitu dhammau aniyatassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

aniyatau haitu dhammau aniyatassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatau haitu dhammau micchattaniyatassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatau haitu dhammau sammattaniyatassa haitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

185. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai pañ‍ca, samanantarai pañ‍ca, sahajātai tīṇi…pai॰… upanissayai satta…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā satta, naārammaṇai satta (saṃkhittaṃ)|
