(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nahaitupadaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

186. micchattaniyataṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca micchattaniyatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

sammattaniyataṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sammattaniyatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sammattaniyataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sammattaniyataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sammattaniyatau nahaitu ca aniyatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

aniyataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

micchattaniyataṃ nahaituñ‍ca aniyataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

sammattaniyataṃ nahaituñ‍ca aniyataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

187. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayau

188. aniyataṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… nauvigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

ārammaṇa-adhipati-anantarapac‍cayā

189. micchattaniyatau nahaitu dhammau aniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sammattaniyatau nahaitu dhammau  aniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau nahaitu dhammau aniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatau nahaitu dhammau micchattaniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatau nahaitu dhammau sammattaniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

190. micchattaniyatau nahaitu dhammau micchattaniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

sammattaniyatau nahaitu dhammau sammattaniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sammattaniyatau  nahaitu dhammau aniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sammattaniyatau nahaitu dhammau sammattaniyatassa nahaitussa ca aniyatassa nahaitussa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

aniyatau  nahaitu dhammau aniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatau nahaitu dhammau sammattaniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

micchattaniyatau nahaitu dhammau aniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sammattaniyatau nahaitu dhammau aniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau nahaitu dhammau aniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatau nahaitu dhammau micchattaniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatau nahaitu dhammau sammattaniyatassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

191. ārammaṇai  pañ‍ca, adhipatiyā aṭṭha, anantarai pañ‍ca, samanantarai pañ‍ca, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai satta…pai॰… avigatai tairasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā tairasa, naārammaṇai tairasa, naadhipatiyā tairasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayā nahaituyā pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

16-1. maggārammaṇattika-haitudukaṃ

192. maggārammaṇaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

maggahaitukaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

maggādhipatiṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

maggārammaṇaṃ haituñ‍ca maggādhipatiṃ haituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

maggahaitukaṃ haituñ‍ca maggādhipatiṃ haituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

193. haituyā sattarasa…pai॰… avigatai sattarasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatiyā  sattarasa, napuraijātai sattarasa…pai॰… naāsaivanai nava, navipākai sattarasa, navippayuttai sattarasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

194. maggārammaṇau haitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

maggahaitukau  haitu dhammau maggahaitukassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

maggādhipati haitu dhammau maggādhipatissa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca|

maggārammaṇau haitu ca maggādhipati haitu ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

maggahaitukau haitu ca maggādhipati haitu ca dhammā maggahaitukassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

maggahaitukau haitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggahaitukau haitu dhammau maggādhipatissa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggahaitukau haitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa haitussa ca maggādhipatissa haitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

maggādhipati  haitu dhammau maggādhipatissa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

maggahaitukau haitu ca maggādhipati haitu ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

195. maggārammaṇau haitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

maggahaitukau haitu dhammau  maggahaitukassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca|

maggādhipati haitu dhammau maggādhipatissa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca|

maggārammaṇau  haitu ca maggādhipati haitu ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

maggahaitukau haitu ca maggādhipati haitu ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca|

maggārammaṇau haitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

maggādhipati haitu dhammau maggādhipatissa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

maggārammaṇau haitu ca maggādhipati haitu ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

196. haituyā sattarasa, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā aikavīsa, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai sattarasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai sattarasa, nissayai sattarasa, upanissayai aikavīsa…pai॰… avigatai sattarasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā aikavīsa, naārammaṇai sattarasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai sattarasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nahaitupadaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

197. maggārammaṇaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| maggārammaṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| maggārammaṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau nahaitu ca maggādhipati nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

maggahaitukaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| maggahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| maggahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau nahaitu ca maggādhipati nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

maggādhipati nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| maggādhipatiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| maggādhipatiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| maggādhipatiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau nahaitu ca maggādhipati nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| maggādhipatiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau nahaitu ca maggādhipati nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

maggārammaṇaṃ nahaituñ‍ca maggādhipatiṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| maggārammaṇaṃ nahaituñ‍ca maggādhipatiṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati  nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| maggārammaṇaṃ nahaituñ‍ca maggādhipatiṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau nahaitu ca maggādhipati nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

maggahaitukaṃ nahaituñ‍ca maggādhipatiṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| maggahaitukaṃ nahaituñ‍ca maggādhipatiṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipatiṃ nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| maggahaitukaṃ nahaituñ‍ca maggādhipatiṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau nahaitu ca maggādhipati nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

198. haituyā  sattarasa, ārammaṇai sattarasa…pai॰… avigatai sattarasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayau

199. maggārammaṇaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā sattarasa, napuraijātai sattarasa, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai nava, navipākai sattarasa, navippayuttai sattarasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā sattarasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

200. maggahaitukau nahaitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa  ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggahaitukau nahaitu dhammau maggādhipatissa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggahaitukau nahaitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa ca maggādhipatissa nahaitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

maggādhipati nahaitu dhammau maggādhipatissa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggādhipati nahaitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggādhipati nahaitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa ca maggādhipatissa nahaitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

maggahaitukau nahaitu ca maggādhipati nahaitu ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

201. maggārammaṇau  nahaitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

maggahaitukau  nahaitu dhammau maggahaitukassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggahaitukau nahaitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggahaitukau nahaitu dhammau maggādhipatissa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggahaitukau nahaitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa ca maggādhipatissa nahaitussa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggahaitukau nahaitu dhammau maggahaitukassa nahaitussa ca maggādhipatissa nahaitussa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

maggādhipati nahaitu dhammau maggādhipatissa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca|

maggārammaṇau nahaitu ca maggādhipati nahaitu ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

maggahaitukau nahaitu ca maggādhipati nahaitu ca dhammā maggahaitukassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca|

anantara-upanissayapac‍cayā

202. maggārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau maggādhipatissa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggārammaṇau  nahaitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa ca maggādhipatissa nahaitussa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

maggādhipati nahaitu dhammau maggādhipatissa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggādhipati nahaitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggādhipati nahaitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa ca maggādhipatissa nahaitussa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

maggārammaṇau nahaitu ca maggādhipati nahaitu ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi…pai॰…|

203. maggārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

maggahaitukau  nahaitu dhammau maggahaitukassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca|

maggādhipati nahaitu dhammau maggādhipatissa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca|

maggārammaṇau nahaitu ca maggādhipati nahaitu ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa nahaitussa  dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

maggahaitukau nahaitu ca maggādhipati nahaitu ca dhammā maggahaitukassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

204. ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā aikavīsa, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai sattarasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai sattarasa, nissayai sattarasa, upanissayai aikavīsa…pai॰… avigatai sattarasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā aikavīsa, naārammaṇai sattarasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

17-1. uppan‍nattika-haitudukaṃ

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayādi

205. uppan‍nau  haitu dhammau uppan‍nassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uppan‍nā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ haitūnaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

anuppan‍nau haitu dhammau uppan‍nassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anuppan‍nā haitū caitaupariyañāṇassa anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| uppādī haitu dhammau uppan‍nassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uppādī haitū caitaupariyañāṇassa anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

uppan‍nau  haitu dhammau uppan‍nassa haitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

206. haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā tīṇi, sahajātai aikaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, upanissayai dvai…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā dvai, naārammaṇai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nahaitupadaṃ

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

207. uppan‍nau  nahaitu dhammau uppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anuppan‍nau nahaitu dhammau uppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

uppādī nahaitu dhammau uppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

208. uppan‍nau nahaitu dhammau uppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anuppan‍nau nahaitu dhammau uppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

uppādī nahaitu dhammau  uppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

uppan‍nau  nahaitu dhammau uppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

anuppan‍nau nahaitu dhammau uppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

uppādī nahaitu dhammau uppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

209. ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, sahajātai aikaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, upanissayai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā tīṇi, naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayā nahaituyā tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

210. pac‍cuppan‍nau  haitu dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

atītau haitu dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| anāgatau haitu dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

211. haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… sahajātai aikaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, upanissayai dvai…pai॰… vipākai aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

213. ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… sahajātai aikaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, upanissayai tīṇi…pai॰… āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai dvai, vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… indriyai aikaṃ, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

19-1. atītārammaṇattika-haitudukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

214. atītārammaṇaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

anāgatārammaṇaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anāgatārammaṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

215. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatiyā  tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

216. atītārammaṇau  haitu dhammau atītārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anāgatārammaṇau haitu dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau haitu dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

atītārammaṇau haitu dhammau atītārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| atītārammaṇau haitu dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa  haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| atītārammaṇau haitu dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

anāgatārammaṇau haitu dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau haitu dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa haitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

217. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā satta, anantarai cha, samanantarai cha, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā nava, naārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nahaitupadaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

218. atītārammaṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

anāgatārammaṇaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anāgatārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

219. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā  tīṇi, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

220. atītārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau atītārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

anāgatārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

haitupac‍cayau

283. abyākataṃ  nahaituṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaituyā aikaṃ…pai॰… nauvigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

284. abyākatau nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau abyākatassa nahaitussa ahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

ārammaṇai aikaṃ, adhipatiyā aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

1. kusalaṃ sappac‍cayaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau sappac‍cayau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ sappac‍cayaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sappac‍cayau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ sappac‍cayaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau sappac‍cayau  ca abyākatau sappac‍cayau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

akusalaṃ sappac‍cayañ‍ca abyākataṃ sappac‍cayañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sappac‍cayau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

7. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

8. kusalau saṃkilaisikau dhammau akusalassa saṃkilaisikassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

akusalau saṃkilaisikau dhammau akusalassa saṃkilaisikassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

abyākatau  saṃkilaisikau dhammau abyākatassa saṃkilaisikassa dhammassa  haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

9. haituyā satta, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai tairasa (saṃkhittaṃ| yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

10. kusalaṃ asaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau asaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ asaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau asaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ|)

104. saikkhaṃ araṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saikkhau araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saikkhaṃ araṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saikkhaṃ araṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saikkhau araṇau ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau araṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

asaikkhaṃ araṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaikkhau araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ araṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)
